  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式视频生成多强调“听指令”，而源文本/知识对齐方法又偏重外部叙事一致性；在交互续写时，用户意图、外部来源与已生成片段之间存在潜在依赖，单看任一输入都无法捕捉，导致上下文选取与整合缺位。

Method: 将“情境落地”表述为把异构输入转化为可执行的约束模型：1) 结构化上下文抽象（SCA），恢复已落地状态与跨上下文依赖；2) 生成约束归纳（GCI），把相关依赖投射为候选特定的约束；3) 受约束的视频生成（CVG），通过规划—校验—修订—渲染循环强制执行约束。框架适配三种视频生成主干。

Result: 在三种生成骨干上均拿到最高综合分，相比各骨干最强基线平均绝对提升10.3分；在叙事与知识双维度都有提升；但在依赖抽取与渲染阶段的忠实实现仍有挑战。

Conclusion: 将交互式续写的多源上下文转译为显式可执行约束可系统提升一致性与可控性；VIS-Ground验证了该思路的有效性，同时暴露出依赖识别与约束忠实渲染的待解问题。

Abstract: Video interactive storytelling enables viewers to actively steer how a video unfolds. However, once we allow viewers to intervene during generation, a new challenge arises: The viewer's request can have latent dependencies on both the grounding source and the current rendered video state. These dependencies may not be explicitly stated in any individual input, but emerge only when the source, rendered history, and new viewer intent are considered jointly. Existing interactive video generation systems primarily emphasize following viewer instructions, while source-grounded video generation methods focus on aligning generated content with an external narrative or knowledge source. This leaves a fundamental question underexplored: What context should a generation model ground on during interactive continuation, and how can heterogeneous, unstructured inputs be transformed into such grounding context? In this work, we formulate contextual grounding as the process of transforming heterogeneous input context into an executable constraint model for video generation. To address this challenge, we introduce VIS-Ground, which performs Structured Context Abstraction to recover grounded states and cross-context dependencies, Generation Constraints Induction to project relevant dependencies into candidate-specific constraints, and Constrained Video Generation to enforce these constraints through planning, verification, revision, and rendering. Across three video generation backbones, VIS-Ground consistently achieves the highest overall composite score, reaching an average absolute improvement of 10.3 points over the strongest per-backbone baselines. Detailed analysis further shows gains across both narrative and knowledge grounding, and reveals remaining challenges in dependency extraction, and faithful realization during video rendering.

</details>


### [37] [Visual Jev Rewards: Reference-Bound Verification for Multi-Subject Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09328)
*Baoteng Li,Wenzhuo Wu,Kongming Liang,Zhanyu Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出“Visual Jev”参考绑定奖励，用问答式二分类校验指定主体的属性/动作/关系是否与给定参考身份共同成立，并将“Yes”概率均值作为GRPO训练奖励，提升多主体图像生成的一致性与可评查性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多主体生成不仅要检测主体是否出现，还要核验是否是“正确的参考主体”参与了指定交互与关系。现有奖励常忽略身份绑定，导致误判与训练信号噪声。需要一种能同时约束条件成立与身份匹配的、可读可查的奖励机制。

Method: 离线构建固定问题集；用参考图像与文本条件生成与主体相关的判定问题。训练一个Qwen3.5-4B作为二分类验证器，仅以二元监督学习。推理时直接从LM头读取“Yes”概率；对每个任务的多个问题取均值作为GRPO奖励，同时保留各子判断以便人工审查。用MICo-150K的200个训练任务、30次更新进行优化；比较与直接用27B打分器的奖励。

Result: 在手工挑选的897个MICo-Bench子集上，GPT-5.4复合分从41.78提升到52.50；使用27B直接评分作奖励得到51.84。每种奖励各进行一次GRPO跑；离线人工评测未显示相对直接评分具有统计显著优势。

Conclusion: Visual Jev实现了参考绑定的可解释奖励，在多主体图像生成上带来一定提升，但优势相对直接大模型评分尚未统计显著；方法提供了可审计的判定粒度与简化的奖励读出，值得进一步在更大规模与更稳健评测下验证。

Abstract: Multi-subject image generation requires rewards that verify whether requested attributes, actions, and relations hold for the specified reference subjects. Subject presence alone does not establish that the correct subjects participate in a requested interaction. We present reference-bound Visual Jev rewards that turn these visual decisions into generator training signals. Each subject-related question receives a positive label only when the requested condition and the relevant reference identities hold jointly. We construct fixed questions offline, train a Qwen3.5-4B verifier with binary supervision, and directly read Yes probabilities from its language-model head. Their mean supplies a GRPO reward while retaining individual judgments for inspection. Using 200 MICo-150K training tasks and 30 updates, the framework raises a GPT-5.4 composite score from 41.78 to 52.50 on a manually selected 897-task MICo-Bench subset; direct 27B rewards yield 51.84. Each reward is tested in one GRPO run, and offline human evaluation does not establish a statistically significant advantage over direct scoring. The study provides an initial implementation and evaluation of Visual Jev as a reference-bound reward for multi-subject image generation.

</details>


### [38] [GRC-Net: Global Representation Consistency Network for Unsupervised Multimodal Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09329)
*Seyoung Jeong,Jong Pil Yun,Sang Jun Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GRC-Net，用全局注意力MLP确保跨patch的一致表征，并配合稳定重建模块，减少正常区域重建噪声；在MVTec 3D-AD与Eyecandies上于图像与像素层面均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯2D图像方法难以检测结构/几何异常，多模态含3D信息虽有效，但主流方法依赖局部patch表征，正常区域也易出现不稳定重建误差，影响异常检测的可靠性与阈值稳健性。

Method: 提出GRC-Net：1) 全局注意力MLP与全局token对所有patch嵌入进行全局一致性建模，提升上下文整体性；2) 稳定重建模块，通过最小化原始与预测嵌入差异来抑制重建噪声，获得稳定的重建误差；3) 将上述用于多模态（含3D）缺陷检测流程，在图像与像素层面评估。

Result: 在MVTec 3D-AD与Eyecandies数据集上，GRC-Net在图像级与像素级指标上均显著超越现有方法；定性分析显示正常区域重建误差明显降低，正常/异常区域的重建差异更清晰。

Conclusion: 全局一致性建模结合稳定重建能改善多模态缺陷检测的鲁棒性与判别性，缓解局部patch方法带来的不稳定误差，并在标准基准上取得领先表现。

Abstract: Automated quality inspection is essential for ensuring product reliability in manufacturing.While image-based methods effectively capture appearance-related defects, these methods are limited in detecting structural and geometric anomalies, motivating multimodal approaches incorporating 3D information. However, existing methods mainly rely on local patch-level representations, which often lead to unstable reconstruction errors even in normal regions. To address this limitation, we propose GRC-Net, which integrates a global-attention MLP to enforce global representation consistency across patch embeddings with a stable reconstruction module to improve reconstruction stability. The proposed method captures holistic contextual information through a global token and suppresses reconstruction noise by minimizing discrepancies between original and predicted embeddings. Experiments on MVTec 3D-AD and Eyecandies demonstrate that GRC-Net consistently outperforms existing methods at both image and pixel levels. Qualitative results further demonstrate reduced reconstruction errors in normal regions and more distinct reconstruction differences between normal and anomalous regions.

</details>


### [39] [CRT-HMAR: Causal Requirement Tracing-Guided Hierarchical Multi-Agent Regulation for Open-Task-Aware Infrared-Visible Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09330)
*Zengyi Yang,Shuai Yuan,Zhong-Cheng Wu,Juan Cheng,Huafeng Li,Yu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CRT-HMAR：一种面向开放任务的红外-可见光图像融合分层多智能体因果需求引导框架，利用因果需求追踪定位任务偏好，结合历史分析的多目标平衡与任务级冲突缓解，实现“需求解释—任务平衡—冲突缓解”的层级调控，在五种下游任务的开放场景中显著提升对未见任务的泛化，同时保持已见任务的性能与平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IR-VIS融合方法多为固定任务集内的任务定制，难以泛化到未见任务，限制了在真实开放任务场景中的适用性。需要一种能从任务需求层面出发、可适配多任务并具备对新任务泛化能力的融合框架。

Method: 提出CRT-HMAR框架：1) 因果需求追踪的任务定位，通过主动干预关键图像信息并观察任务网络响应变化，得到任务特定的图像级因果需求图；2) 基于需求图的需求分析智能体，汇聚任务特定需求以自适应引导定制化融合；3) 历史分析的多目标平衡与任务级纠偏的冲突缓解机制，构建“需求解释—任务平衡—冲突缓解”的分层调控链；4) 多智能体协作，动态调控开放任务需求建模、多任务平衡优化与梯度冲突缓解等关键过程。

Result: 在包含五种下游任务的开放任务场景中进行大量实验，CRT-HMAR在未见任务上的泛化能力显著提升，同时维持对已见任务的性能与任务间平衡。

Conclusion: 将IR-VIS融合范式由面向任务的建模转向面向需求的建模，通过层级化多智能体与因果需求追踪实现对开放任务的适配与泛化，推动从封闭任务设置走向真实世界开放任务应用。

Abstract: Infrared and visible (IR-VIS) image fusion integrates complementary multimodal information into a single fused image to support downstream vision tasks. However, existing methods are typically tailored to seen tasks within a fixed task set and struggle to generalize to unseen tasks, which restricts their applicability in real-world open-task scenarios. To address this issue, this paper proposes CRT-HMAR, a Causal Requirement Tracing-Guided Hierarchical Multi-Agent Regulation Framework for open-task-aware IR-VIS image fusion. CRT-HMAR introduces a Causal Requirement Tracing Task Localization mechanism, which actively intervenes in key image information and observes task-network response variations to map task-specific semantic preferences into image-level causal requirement maps. Based on these maps, a requirement analysis agent aggregates task-specific requirement knowledge to adaptively guide requirement-customized image fusion. Moreover, CRT-HMAR incorporates History-Analysis Multi-Objective Balancing and Task-Level-Correction Conflict Mitigation mechanisms, jointly constructing a hierarchical regulation chain of "requirement interpretation - task balancing - conflict mitigation". Through multiple collaborative agents, CRT-HMAR dynamically regulates key processes including open-task requirement modeling, multi-task balanced optimization, and gradient conflict mitigation. Extensive experiments on open-task scenarios involving five downstream tasks demonstrate that CRT-HMAR significantly improves generalization to unseen tasks while maintaining the performance and balance of seen tasks. Overall, CRT-HMAR shifts IR-VIS image fusion from task-oriented modeling toward requirement-oriented modeling, promoting its extension from closed-task settings to real-world open-task scenarios.

</details>


### [40] [TileSkipper: Region-Adaptive Tile Pruning for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09343)
*Jingxing Li,Yongjae Lee,Deliang Fan,Abhay Kumar Yadav,Cheng Peng,Rama Chellappa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TileSkipper：为3D高斯溅射(3DGS)平铺光栅化器学习静态、按高斯分组的截断阈值策略，以减少无效tile贡献而不显著降质，达到纯编译期/推理期零开销的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有平铺3DGS常用全场统一截断阈值，但不同高斯对支持截断的敏感度差异大；统一阈值要么浪费算力要么引入失真。需要一种更细粒度而又运行时零开销的策略来在速度与质量间更好折中。

Method: - 将高斯分为64组，为每个高斯学习一个静态一字节“截断策略/阈值”，无运行时推理或参数更新。
- 通过“校准渲染”评估候选策略：同时统计tile配对节省与失真代理（考虑前向透射率与背景色），并在互斥的选择视角上完成整图渲染后才接受策略，避免过拟合。
- 导出策略后仅以字节标注参与渲染；兼容现有不透明度感知边界与3σ栅格器。

Result: 在13个场景（Mip-NeRF 360、Tanks & Temples、Deep Blending）上，固定策略的AccuTile扫描：标准分辨率平均加速1.088×，宽3840像素时1.238×，PSNR平均变化-0.007/-0.023 dB。与6种现有不透明度边界集成可获得仅编译期的1.009×–1.121×提速。对来自3σ栅格器的4个移植，区分了既有精确边界带来的收益与本方法的增量收益。

Conclusion: 静态、按高斯分配的一字节截断策略在不引入运行时额外核或推理的情况下，能在多数据集上稳定带来小而稳健的加速且几乎无感知质量损失；与现有边界方法可叠加，较全局阈值与场景级校准更优，和AdaGScale对比则依赖具体工况。

Abstract: Tiled 3D Gaussian Splatting rasterizers often use one scene-wide contribution cutoff for tile enumeration, although content differs in its sensitivity to support truncation. TileSkipper selects a static per-Gaussian cutoff policy for a frozen checkpoint. Calibration renders measure candidate pair savings and an isolated-removal distortion proxy that accounts for front transmittance and background color. The method allocates cutoffs across 64 Gaussian groups and accepts policies only after complete renders on disjoint selection views. The exported policy uses one byte per Gaussian, with no parameter updates, additional kernel, or per-frame policy inference. Across 13 scenes from Mip-NeRF 360, Tanks & Temples, and Deep Blending, a fixed-policy AccuTile sweep gives dataset-macro speedups of $1.088\times$ at standard resolution and $1.238\times$ at 3840 pixels wide, with $-0.007/-0.023$ dB mean PSNR change. Six integrations with existing opacity-aware bounds yield $1.009\times$--$1.121\times$ compiler-only speedups. For four ports from $3σ$ rasterizers, we separately attribute the prior exact-bound transition and our incremental gain. Matched-quality ablations show modest gains over scene-global calibration and parity with per-Gaussian control; the standalone comparison with AdaGScale is regime-dependent.

</details>


### [41] [Do Image Editors Follow Depth-Dependent Blur and Aperture Response? A Rendered-Ground-Truth Pilot Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09344)
*Zhihan Chen,Yuhuan Zhao,Yijie Zhu,Xinyu Yao,Mengcong Ren,Yuchen Sun,Yunqing Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 对两款图像编辑器的“光圈虚化”能力做小规模审计：它们添加的景深模糊既不随景深位置按薄透镜比例扩散，也不随光圈变化呈正确倍比，整体显著“压缩”了深度与光圈响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际图像编辑常被要求把照片做成f/1.4效果，但很少检验其是否符合物理薄透镜光学。已有评测多用单一或混合指标，无法在已知深度处分别报告“深度比例”和“光圈响应”两项关键物理属性，导致对编辑器是否“像真实相机”缺乏可解释结论。

Method: 构建可控的渲染“真值”基准：用Blender Cycles渲染具有真实薄透镜景深的三联画场景，共24种纹理；用同一模糊宽度估计器一致地测量真值与编辑器输出；预注册深度位置与光圈设定；测试两款编辑器（Gemini 3.1 Flash Image、GPT-image-2.5），并进行黑箱提示词干预（定性描述、像素级模糊强度、f值表述等）。

Result: 与真值相比，两编辑器的模糊随光圈变化的倍比显著不足：f/1.4到f/2.8的模糊宽度比仅0.99–1.18（真值应≈1.98–2.13）。近/远景模糊的中位数比值也被压缩：Gemini≈1.18–1.25、GPT-image≈0.97–1.05（真值≈1.69–1.82）。提示词实验显示：定性描述可使模糊强度变化约2–10倍；直接指定2与6像素仅带来1.1–1.3倍；任何f值表述都未达到“遵循”标准。深度压缩在正反中心对焦布局中均存在；近景对焦时深度指数可达阈值，但光圈响应始终衰减。改编自文献的标量指标会对“比例被压缩的合成编辑器”给出类似真值的高分，提示这些指标可能误导。

Conclusion: 当前通用图像编辑器未能物理一致地复现薄透镜景深，其深度与光圈响应均被系统性压缩；常用单标量评测可能掩盖此问题。应在已知深度下分别报告深度比例与光圈响应，改进提示与训练，或引入物理一致的渲染/生成约束，以实现可信的“f/1.4风格”编辑。

Abstract: General image editors are asked to make a photo look as if it were taken at f/1.4, yet it is rarely checked whether the blur they add follows thin-lens optics. A physical aperture edit spreads blur across depth in thin-lens proportions and changes the blur when the aperture changes; prior evaluations check blur monotonicity, sharpness-trend correlation, effective-aperture error, or vision-language judgments, and none we found reports the two properties separately at known depths. In this pilot audit of two editors (Gemini~3.1 Flash Image and GPT-image-2.5) we compare against a rendered oracle: Blender Cycles scenes with true thin-lens depth of field, one blur-width estimator applied identically to oracle and editor outputs, and preregistered depth and aperture indices. In 24 texture scenes rendered in one three-panel geometry, accepted and measurable panels show f/1.4-to-f/2.8 width ratios, $\barσ_{1.4}/\barσ_{2.8}$, of 0.99--1.18 against 1.98--2.13 for the oracle, and ratios of pooled median Gaussian-equivalent near/far blur widths of about 1.18--1.25 (Gemini) and 0.97--1.05 (GPT-image) against 1.69--1.82. Preregistered black-box interventions show that qualitative wording changes blur strength by roughly 2--10 times, whereas a request for 2 versus 6 px changes it 1.1--1.3 times and no tested wording of the f-number meets the registered ``followed'' criterion. The depth compression appears in the original and reversed centre-focus layouts; with the focus on the near panel, the available-panel depth index reaches the registered threshold, and the aperture response stays attenuated in every layout tested. Scalar metrics adapted from published ones give oracle-like scores to synthetic editors whose proportions are compressed.

</details>


### [42] [Closing the Loop on Contrail Avoidance with Satellite Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09363)
*Spandan Ghose Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 小型扩散模型用于卫星图像中航迹云检测，显著优于常见基线；提高CNN输入分辨率和简单的数据增强可达相当水平；针对形状的预训练会造成严重过拟合与精度崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航迹云占航空变暖的重要份额，若能确认并规避少量高影响航班，可显著降低变暖。实现这一点需要卫星可靠判定“未形成”的航迹云，但其极窄、稀疏且与自然卷云相似，现有检测困难。

Method: 构建8.4M参数、单GPU可训的小型扩散模型用于像素级检测；与DeepLabV3+和改造的MedSegDiff对比；通过受控消融研究输入分辨率、数据增强（翻转/旋转）、以及在合成/形状先验上的预训练影响。

Result: 扩散模型PR-AUC=0.476，优于DeepLabV3+(0.414)与MedSegDiff(0.119)；将CNN输入分辨率加倍可达0.499（与扩散模型接近，p=0.07）。简单的翻转与旋转增强使准确率提升超过一倍，影响大于架构差异；对航迹形状的预训练导致模型在空场景“补画”细线，精度降至1%，且阈值或引导均无法挽救。

Conclusion: 在超窄目标稀疏检测中：1) 先审视输入分辨率再设计新架构；2) 简单几何增强收益巨大；3) 针对目标形状的预训练可能有害并误导评价，召回主导的指标会掩盖精度崩溃。对航迹云检测与更广泛遥感/医学等细结构分割具启示。

Abstract: Contrails are the thin ice clouds that aircraft leave behind. They cause a large share of aviation's warming, and rerouting the few flights that produce them could avoid much of it. However, an avoided contrail only counts if a satellite can confirm that it never formed, and this check is hard: contrails are one to two pixels wide, cover only 0.18% of pixels, and look very similar to natural cirrus. We build a small diffusion model (8.4M parameters, trained on one GPU) that detects them, and we run a controlled study to find out which components matter. The model reaches 0.476 PR-AUC, compared with 0.414 for a DeepLabV3+ baseline and 0.119 for an adapted MedSegDiff. Doubling the input resolution of the CNN brings it to parity (0.499, p=0.07). Three lessons apply beyond contrails. First, check the input resolution before designing a new architecture. Second, simple flips and rotations more than double accuracy and matter more than any architectural choice we measured. Third, pretraining the model on contrail shapes is harmful: the model learns that thin strokes appear everywhere and paints them onto empty scenes. Precision collapses to 1% while recall-based metrics still rate the degraded model as excellent, and no threshold or guidance heuristic repairs this failure.

</details>


### [43] [Unified Multi-plane Autoregressive Diffusion for 3D Multi-contrast MRI Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09376)
*Yejee Shin,Geonhui Son,Jinglu Wang,Minwoo Jung,Yan Lu,Dosik Hwang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MPAD：在3D潜空间中用2D扩散实现全容积MRI对比度合成，兼顾体素一致性与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多对比度MRI有诊断价值但采集耗时；希望用已获取的对比度合成缺失对比度。问题本质为3D，需要跨轴向/矢状/冠状一致，但全3D生成模型计算与内存成本过高。

Method: 先用3D自编码器将体数据压缩为各向同性3D潜表示；在潜空间中训练2D扩散模型：给定源对比度切片与部分未遮挡的目标对比度切片，重建被遮挡的目标切片。推理时采用多平面自回归：在一个方向内随机顺序自回归生成切片以保持面内连续性，并将已生成的切片作为条件先验传播到正交方向以强化面间一致性，实现全体3D合成。

Result: 相较3D潜扩散基线，训练FLOPs降7倍、推理FLOPs降3倍，同时降低推理时间与峰值显存；在多数据集上取得更优图像质量，并能在单一模型内实现一对多翻译。

Conclusion: MPAD以2D操作达成3D一致的MRI合成，在显著降低算力与内存的同时提升质量与灵活性，适合资源受限环境下的临床级多对比度重建/合成。

Abstract: Acquiring a complete set of magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) contrasts is time-intensive and uncomfortable for patients, despite the diagnostic value of multi-contrast imaging. This motivates synthesizing missing contrasts from those already acquired, which is an inherently 3D problem requiring anatomical coherence across axial, sagittal, and coronal planes. However, fully 3D generative models are often impracti- cal under computational resources that scale cubically with volume size. We propose a unified Multi-Plane Autoregressive Diffusion (MPAD), a latent diffusion framework that achieves full-volume 3D synthesis using efficient plane-wise 2D operations while preserving volumetric coherence. A 3D autoencoder first compresses MRI scans into an isotropic 3D la- tent representation. A 2D diffusion model is then trained to reconstruct masked latent slices of the target contrast, conditioned on both source- contrast slices and unmasked target-contrast slices. During inference, we introduce plane-wise autoregressive synthesis with inter-plane priors. Slices are generated autoregressively in random order within one plane orientation to maintain intra-plane continuity, then propagated as con- ditioning priors to orthogonal plane orientations to enforce inter-plane consistency. Compared to 3D latent diffusion baselines, MPAD reduces training and inference FLOPs by 7x and 3x, respectively, while also lowering inference time and peak memory consumption. Experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate that MPAD achieves superior perfor- mance, generating high-fidelity 3D volumes and supporting one-to-many translation within a single unified model.

</details>


### [44] [PPCAR-Net: Projection-Refined Parametric 3D Coronary Artery Reconstruction from Sparse X-ray Angiographic Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09383)
*Yu Ren,Hwee Kuan Lee,Tat-Jen Cham,Jonathan Yap,Khung Keong Yeo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PPCAR-Net：从少视角血管造影分割直接预测分支化“中线+半径”参数表示，通过投影引导细化，无需匹配/三角化/体素重建，实现实时高保真冠脉3D重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有少视角3D冠脉重建要么依赖跨视角对应与三角化，易受遮挡与短缩影响；要么先做体素/体积预测再提取血管图，但不能直接得到中线与半径，且易碎裂。需要一种能在多视角变化下稳健、直接输出中心线与半径、并能实时运行的方法。

Method: 提出PPCAR-Net：1) 输入为变数量的分割视图；2) 粗预测器将冻结的VGGT特征与可学习分支查询结合，直接输出每条分支的存在性、B样条中心线轨迹、以及致密半径曲线；3) 投影引导的几何与半径细化器，从原始视图中采样局部证据，利用3D监督学习残差校正；4) 采用粗到细推理流程。

Result: 在基于CT冠脉解剖生成的仿真造影掩膜上，PPCAR-Net重建连通性更好，中线精度（尤其RCA）更强，同时体积重叠度具竞争力；推理耗时约121 ms，可实时。

Conclusion: PPCAR-Net无需点匹配、三角化或体素中间体，直接预测分支化中心线与半径，并通过投影引导细化提升精度，兼顾连通性、中心线精度与速度，适用于稀疏视角的实时冠脉3D重建。

Abstract: Sparse-view 3D coronary reconstruction commonly relies on cross-view correspondence and triangulation, which are vulnerable to vessel overlap and foreshortening, or on volumetric prediction followed by vascular-graph extraction, which does not directly provide centrelines and radii. We introduce PPCAR-Net, a projection-refined parametric coronary artery reconstruction network that directly predicts a branch-structured centreline-and-radius representation without explicit point matching, triangulation, or an intermediate volume. Given a variable number of segmented views, a coarse predictor combines frozen VGGT features with learned branch queries to estimate branch presence, B-spline centreline trajectories, and dense radius profiles. Projection-guided geometry and radius refiners then sample local evidence from the input views and apply residual corrections learned with 3D supervision. We evaluate representation fidelity and sparse-view reconstruction quantitatively and qualitatively. On simulated angiographic masks generated from CT-derived coronary anatomy, PPCAR-Net produces better connected artery reconstructions and achieves strong centreline accuracy, particularly for RCA, while maintaining competitive volumetric overlap. Coarse-to-fine inference takes 121 ms, enabling real-time reconstruction.

</details>


### [45] [Quantifying Volumetric Risk: Class-Aware Asymmetric Weighted Conformal Prediction for 3D Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09392)
*Shadi Alijani,Fereshteh Aghaee Meibodi,Homayoun Najjaran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CA-WCP：一种面向3D多类分割的类感知非对称加权保序预测，实现在分布偏移下的校准体素体积区间与更窄的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学3D体积分割（如脑肿瘤）在临床需要可靠且可校准的不确定性，现有基础模型（如MedSAM）是确定性的；2D/对称假设的保序预测难以应对3D多类任务中类特异的偏差与协变量移位。

Method: 提出Class-Aware Asymmetric Weighted Conformal Prediction (CA-WCP)：1) 在潜空间用密度比加权处理协变量移位；2) 对体积下/上界分别用方向性分位数；3) 依据验证集的FP/FN率为每类学习非对称缩放因子；4) 证明对每个类别保持加权可交换性下的边际覆盖保证；并将校准区间编码为结构化提示，供多模态大模型生成不确定性条件的放射报告。

Result: 在BraTS 2020与合成多器官CT移位基准上，各类别的实测覆盖的95% Clopper–Pearson区间均包含名义90%水平；相较对称加权CP，区间宽度缩小8–14%。

Conclusion: CA-WCP在分布偏移下为3D多类分割提供类级别、非对称且更紧的覆盖保证，并可将不确定性转化为可解释的临床报告提示。

Abstract: Reliable volumetric segmentation is critical for clinical diagnostics, yet foundation models such as MedSAM remain deterministic and lack calibrated uncertainty under distribution shift. Existing conformal prediction methods offer statistical guarantees but are frequently applied in 2D and assume symmetric error distributions, so they do not capture the class-specific biases that arise in 3D multi-class segmentation. We propose Class-Aware Asymmetric Weighted Conformal Prediction (CA-WCP), which combines latent-space density-ratio weighting for covariate shift with directional quantiles for the lower and upper volume bounds, and scales each bound by a class-specific asymmetry factor derived from validation-set false-positive and false-negative rates. We prove that CA-WCP retains the weighted-exchangeability marginal coverage guarantee for every class, and we evaluate it on 3D brain tumor segmentation (BraTS 2020) and on a synthetic multi-organ CT benchmark constructed under covariate shift. On both benchmarks the 95\% Clopper--Pearson interval for the observed coverage of CA-WCP contains the nominal 90\% level for every semantic class, while interval width is reduced by 8--14\% relative to symmetric weighted conformal prediction. We further encode the calibrated intervals into structured prompts for a multimodal large language model to produce uncertainty-conditioned radiology reports, linking distribution-shift-aware uncertainty quantification to interpretable clinical communication.

</details>


### [46] [One Frame, Full Heartbeat: ECG-Free Cardiac Cine MRI Synthesis via Phase-Conditioned Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09397)
*Shiyi Wang,Ruochen Sun,Peirong Liu,Xiang Li,Fangxu Xing*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhaseFlow提出一种无需ECG的生成式框架，通过相位估计与流模型合成完整心动周期 cine CMR，重建出高保真动力学与图像质量，并在ACDC上超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多帧cine CMR依赖ECG门控与多次屏气，易受患者配合与资源限制影响；现有合成方法要么显式依赖ECG、要么缺乏生理约束，难以忠实复现EF与收缩幅度等关键动态指标。

Method: 1) 从分割得到的左心室面积曲线中直接估计非线性心动相位，捕捉收缩/舒张不对称，无需ECG；推理时用病理特异相位模板驱动。2) 以相位和切片位置为条件的rectified flow在潜空间合成完整运动轨迹；3) 解码为可微分同胚位移场，直接对舒张末期像素做形变，避免VAE式重建模糊。

Result: 在ACDC基准上取得最佳的LV体积曲线R^2、SSIM与FID，并通过消融显示各组件均有显著贡献。

Conclusion: PhaseFlow将生理一致的相位建模与条件流生成、可微分同胚形变相结合，无需ECG即可生成高保真、临床相关的cine序列，改善动态指标与图像真实感，并具备模块化可扩展性。

Abstract: Cine cardiovascular magnetic resonance (CMR) analysis relies on multi-frame sequences capturing the full cardiac cycle. However, standard multi-frame acquisition depends heavily on electrocardiogram (ECG) gating and repeated breath-holds, posing challenges in uncooperative populations, resource-limited settings, and temporally corrupted datasets. Existing methods that synthesize full cardiac sequences either rely on explicit ECG signals to parameterize myocardium function, or employ deformable registration without physiological constraints, failing to faithfully reproduce clinically relevant dynamic metrics such as ejection fraction (EF) and ventricular contraction magnitude. We present PhaseFlow, a unified generative framework that overcomes both limitations. PhaseFlow estimates a non-linear cardiac phase signal directly from the input sequence via a segmentation-derived left-ventricular (LV) area curve, capturing the asymmetric dynamics of systole and diastole without any ECG dependency. At inference, this phase signal is provided by a pathology-specific template, informing phase-specific frame generation. A rectified flow model conditioned on the phase and slice position synthesizes the full cardiac motion trajectory in the latent space, decoded into a diffeomorphic displacement field that warps end-diastole pixel intensities directly, eliminating the reconstruction blur often accompanying the variational autoencoder. On the ACDC benchmark, PhaseFlow achieves superior physiological fidelity and image realism, with best LV volume curve $R^2$, structural similarity (SSIM) and generative quality (FID) among all baselines. Ablation studies confirm that each proposed component contributes measurably to the overall performance.

</details>


### [47] [TiTok: Audio-Visual LLM for Multi-Segment Temporal Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09408)
*Eunji Shin,Dahyun Choi,Seungyeon Jo,Yejin Hong,Jiyoung Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TiTok，一种音视大模型，通过时间令牌交织与基于GDPO的解耦多段奖励，实现不定数量事件的精准多段定位；并构建新评测协议与CountF1，SOTA表现（65.7 mIoU/0.58 CountF1）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉或视听模型在未裁剪视频的多段语义定位任务上：视觉-only忽略声学补充信息；视听模型虽结合模态，却常无法校准预测事件数量（count miscalibration），且重叠度指标难衡量计数偏差。

Method: 1) 提出AV-LLM TiTok，可对查询预测任意数量时间段；2) 时间令牌交织（TTI）：在视听序列中显式插入特殊时间token，使输入侧时序感知与输出侧时间预测对齐，从而提升边界精度；3) 强化学习：设计面向多段的解耦奖励（全局、局部、计数、精度、格式），并用Group reward–Decoupled Normalization Policy Optimization（GDPO）优化；4) 评测：基于UnAV-100建立新协议，并提出CountF1用于量化计数失配。

Result: 在AV‑MSG任务上取得65.7 mIoU与0.58 CountF1，达当前最优；相比仅重叠指标，新增指标能显著揭示计数校准能力。

Conclusion: 通过TTI对齐时序表示与GDPO下的解耦奖励，TiTok在不定数多段定位上显著缓解计数失配并精确划定边界；新评测协议与CountF1为该任务提供更全面衡量，并推动SOTA。

Abstract: Audio-visual multi-segment grounding (AV-MSG) in untrimmed videos, reasoning over audio-visual evidence and predicting multiple segments for a query, is a fundamental problem but remains challenging. Visual-only models overlook complementary acoustic cues, while audio-visual models often fail to calibrate the number of events - a phenomenon we refer to as count miscalibration. We present TiTok, an audio-visual large language model (AV-LLM) that localizes an arbitrary number of temporal event segments for each query. For precise boundary prediction, we introduce the Time Token Interleaving (TTI) method, which explicitly injects special time tokens into the audio-visual stream to align input-side temporal perception with output-side temporal prediction. We further propose decoupled, multi-segment-oriented rewards for reinforcement learning, consisting of global, local, count, precision, and format rewards, optimized with Group reward-Decoupled Normalization Policy Optimization (GDPO). To assess the performance on AV-MSG, we establish a new UnAV-100-based evaluation protocol, and propose the CountF1 metric for quantifying count miscalibration that overlap metrics fail to capture. TiTok reaches 65.7 mIoU and 0.58 CountF1, achieving state-of-the-art performance. Our code is available at this link.

</details>


### [48] [trACT: temporal revelation Airborne Camera Trap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09417)
*Oliver Bimber,Rakesh John Amala Arokia Nathan,Mohamed Youssef,Vinayak Lal Bhatnagar,Ralf Berger,Klaus Hackländer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出trACT：面向消费级无人机的轻量级实时空中监测框架，利用时间最大池化与自监督运动异常检测，在复杂环境下从背景扰动中可靠分离目标运动，并配合预测与云台变焦验证实现多目标平衡与低延迟精确识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机遥感监控常受热/环境杂波、植被动态、伪装及系统时延影响，难以在宽域覆盖下实现对地面微弱运动的稳定发现与精确验证。受猛禽悬停并稳像以分离地表细微运动的启发，作者欲在消费级平台上实现类似能力。

Method: 提出trACT框架：1) 低层信号处理——Temporal Max Pooling（TMP），在滚动时间窗内将难察觉的细微运动转换为稳健的幅值与时间编码；2) 自监督运动异常检测，将由风及机体漂移引起的背景运动与目标运动区分；3) 结合运动预测以补偿机械与处理延迟；4) 自动化云台稳像与光学变焦验证；5) 多目标公平验证与平衡策略；整体轻量、实时，适配自主消费级无人机。

Result: 在真实世界的高密林地野生动物与安防场景中，大量实验显示：该系统能在宽域巡查的同时，可靠地从复杂动态背景中提取目标运动，并实现自主、精确的目标验证。

Conclusion: trACT在复杂环境中有效衔接“广域空中监测”与“高精度自主验证”，证明轻量实时方案可在消费级无人机上实现类似猛禽的稳像捕猎能力，提升监测与安防的实用性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Effective remote monitoring and surveillance using drones are frequently impeded by severe environmental and thermal clutter, dynamic vegetation, target camouflage, and system latency. Drawing inspiration from the hunting strategies of birds of prey that hover and stabilize their vision to isolate subtle ground motion, we introduce trACT (temporal revelation Airborne Camera Trap), a lightweight, real-time aerial robotics framework designed for autonomous consumer drones. The system integrates Temporal Max Pooling (TMP), a low-level signal processing method that transforms imperceptible movement across a rolling integration window into robust value and time encodings, with self-supervised motion anomaly detection to isolate target motion from background environmental motion caused by wind gusts and drone drift. To overcome mechanical and processing delays, trACT combines motion prediction with automated gimbal-stabilized optical zoom verification and equitable multi-target verification balancing. Extensive real-world field experiments in densely forested wildlife habitats and surveillance scenarios demonstrate that trACT successfully bridges the gap between wide-area aerial monitoring and precise, autonomous target verification under challenging operational conditions.

</details>


### [49] [Spatial Latent Reasoning for Embodied Reference Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09418)
*Ling Li,Jianhui Zhong,Wei Liu,Zheng Jiang aand Yuxuan Liu,Jingyu Li,Zhidong Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SLR框架，通过时序化的几何与视觉潜在状态来进行指向手势的视觉指代落地，利用“空间射线状态”和基于奇偶分组的parity pooling构建目标区域特征，训练期用辅助标注、推理期自回归生成，多模型与多数据集上显著提升mIoU与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指向手势落地需要把手部几何（指尖位置、指向方向）与被指物的视觉身份与范围关联。现有方法缺乏对连续潜在推理的恰当中间监督，难以稳定、可解释地将几何与视觉线索整合。作者希望通过任务结构化的潜在状态，提供可学习、可泛化的监督路径。

Method: 提出Spatial Latent Reasoning (SLR)：将监督组织为按序的潜在状态序列。首先用指尖位置与指向方向监督“空间射线状态”；随后生成四个与目标区域特征对齐的视觉状态。为构建视觉目标，提出parity pooling：对区域token做polyphase分组，在四个交织的空间支撑上求均值。训练与推理均以递归方式生成各状态；辅助标注仅在训练期使用。与多种VL大模型骨干结合，通过监督微调实现。

Result: 在EgoPoint-Ground上，相比同骨干的有监督微调，mIoU分别提升：Qwen3.5-4B +2.8、Qwen2.5-VL-7B +17.5、Qwen3-VL-8B +21.1，并在两个困难子集上都有提升；在YouRefIt上以IoU 0.5阈值达77.6%精度，比已报道SOTA高5.2个百分点（尽管评测协议不同）。消融显示：几何+视觉联合监督在标准与相似物体集合上有效；在标准集合上parity pooling优于三种替代池化。

Conclusion: 将指向落地视为由几何到视觉的连续潜在状态推理，并用任务结构化中间监督训练，可显著提升指向手势的目标定位精度与稳健性；parity pooling是有效的目标特征构建方式。代码与资料将开源。

Abstract: Pointing-gesture visual grounding requires connecting hand geometry with the visual identity and extent of a referred object. A central challenge for continuous latent reasoning is how to organize these complementary cues into useful intermediate supervision. We propose Spatial Latent Reasoning (SLR), a framework that structures this supervision around an ordered sequence of geometric and visual states. A spatial ray state is supervised by fingertip position and pointing direction, followed by four states aligned with target-region features. To construct the visual targets, we introduce parity pooling, which applies polyphase grouping to average region tokens on four interleaved spatial supports. All states are generated recurrently during training and inference; auxiliary annotations are required only during training. On EgoPoint-Ground, the framework improves mIoU over same-backbone supervised fine-tuning by 2.8, 17.5, and 21.1 percentage points on Qwen3.5-4B, Qwen2.5-VL-7B, and Qwen3-VL-8B, respectively, with improvements on both hard subsets. On YouRefIt, it achieves 77.6% precision at IoU 0.5, a numerical margin of 5.2 percentage points over the reported state of the art under differing evaluation protocols. Ablations support joint geometric and visual supervision on the standard and similar-object sets, and favor parity over three alternative pooling operators on the standard set. These results support task-structured supervision for continuous pointing grounding. We will release the code and supporting materials.

</details>


### [50] [Event-Aligned Visual Action Reasoning for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09427)
*Xiaomeng Yang,Yushu Wu,Yi Gao,Yuhao Lei,Xuan Zhang,Pu Zhao,Yanzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出事件对齐的视觉-动作推理框架，通过按交互事件而非固定时间步进行想象预测，并加入执行有效性头；在DOMINO上成功率提升10.26个百分点，并在RoboTwin 2.0具竞争力，且可从Level 1迁移到更高等级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型按预设时间间隔进行视觉想象，未显式区分任务关键交互与过渡阶段，导致推理粒度与任务需求不匹配，可能浪费计算并忽视关键状态变化。

Method: 以“事件”为基本单元构建视觉-动作预测：通过事件对齐的监督，让模型在每次滚动想象中更关注关键状态变化、对连接过渡采用更粗粒度；并引入执行有效性头，用于判定预测动作序列中的有效片段，支持分块推理时去除冗余动作。

Result: 在DOMINO基线之上成功率提升10.26个百分点；在RoboTwin 2.0上达到竞争性表现；可从DOMINO Level 1无目标级适配迁移到Level 2/3。

Conclusion: 将视觉前瞻对齐到交互事件能更有效地指导动作生成，兼顾关键事件的细粒度推理与过渡段的高效推进；执行有效性头进一步减少冗余动作，实现更高成功率与更强泛化/迁移能力。

Abstract: World-Action Models (WAMs) utilize future visual prediction as an intermediate reasoning process to guide action generation. However, existing WAMs typically structure visual imagination according to predefined temporal intervals, without explicitly accounting for the different roles of task-critical interactions and connecting transitions. We argue that effective visual foresight should align directly with task-relevant interactions and their corresponding reasoning demands. To this end, we introduce an event-aligned visual action reasoning framework that organizes visual-action prediction around interaction events. Through event-aligned visual-action supervision, WAM learns to generate event-aligned visual context in each imagined rollout, placing greater emphasis on critical state changes that inform action generation. This shapes the visual reasoning granularity according to the underlying interaction dynamics, with detailed reasoning around task-critical events and coarser progression through connecting transitions. Furthermore, we introduce an execution validity head that identifies the valid portion of each predicted action sequence, avoiding redundant actions during chunked inference. Experiments demonstrate a 10.26 percentage point improvement in DOMINO success rate over baseline and competitive performance on RoboTwin 2.0. It also transfers from DOMINO Level 1 to Levels 2 and 3 without target-level adaptation.

</details>


### [51] [SkillCycle: Co-Evolving Agent Policies and Skill Banks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09430)
*Ling Li,Qiuyu Shen,Zheng Jiang,Qinwei Ma,Yuxuan Liu,Zhidong Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SkillCycle提出一种在语言代理中共演化策略与技能库的方法，通过“内化技能—修订规则”的闭环，让外部技能逐步转化为无需推理时技能输入的内生能力，并在WebShop等环境取得SOTA级或更优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当代理通过外部规则/技能得到指导并逐步学会这些能力后，原有规则可能变得冗余、误导或不足；因此需要同时学习策略与自适应地修订监督其学习的技能/规则，实现随能力变化而动态调整的指导。

Method: 提出SkillCycle：在两个阶段之间交替迭代——(1) 固定技能库与路由器进行策略学习（蒸馏外部技能到策略）；(2) 冻结已学策略，利用“蒸馏反馈”的两重作用来定位与编辑规则：令token级上下文差异定位需检视的规则，以交互结果指导其内容与适用性修改。对候选编辑进行规则级与整库级的环境比较评估，通过后才进入下一轮学习。最终目标是减少直到消除推理时对技能输入的依赖。

Result: 在WebShop上，3B模型无需推理时技能输入即可达成74.74%成功率与88.37分，分别较SOTA相对提升0.73%与3.96%。在ALFWorld与WebShop的Cycle 3消融中，无技能（no-skill）成功率相对静态技能库提高10.18%与18.11%，相对单次库更新提高2.41%与2.50%。

Conclusion: 持续随着代理能力变化而修订技能指导，可将外部技能有效转化为策略内生能力，推理时不再需要技能输入；SkillCycle在多环境验证其有效性，并将开放代码、配置、技能库与评测流程。

Abstract: Internalizing external skills changes a language agent's capabilities and, with them, the value of its remaining guidance: rules can become redundant, misleading, or insufficient for newly encountered decisions. This creates a coupled problem of learning from skills and adapting the skills that supervise further learning. We introduce SkillCycle, a framework for co-evolving agent policies and skill banks through a feedback loop between skill internalization and rule revision. Our central contribution is to give distillation feedback a second role: token-level contextual differences help locate rules for inspection, while interaction outcomes guide edits to their content and applicability. SkillCycle alternates between two phases: policy learning with a fixed skill bank and router, and rule revision with a frozen policy. Candidate edits undergo rule-level and whole-bank environment comparisons before they guide the next learning cycle. On WebShop, SkillCycle with a 3B model achieves a success rate of 74.74% and a score of 88.37 without inference-time skill inputs, representing relative improvements of 0.73% and 3.96% over the state-of-the-art (SOTA) model, respectively. In Cycle 3 ablations on ALFWorld and WebShop, SkillCycle's no-skill success rates improve by 10.18% and 18.11% relative to a static skill bank, and by 2.41% and 2.50% relative to a single bank update, respectively. These results show that continually revising skill guidance as the agent's capabilities change helps transform external skills into policy capabilities that require no skill inputs at inference. We will release code, configurations, skill banks, and evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [52] [Controllable Crowd Generation through World-Model Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09438)
*JunGyu Lee,Jisu Shin,Seunghyun Shin,Hae-Gon Jeon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Ctrl-CWM：一个可控的多智能体人群世界模型，将人群生成与运行时控制融合，通过“想象未来”进行规划，支持无再训练地注入新目标，提升逼真度与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仿真多依赖预设控制，难以在运行中灵活适配新用户目标；现实应用（导航/自动驾驶/城市规划）需要人群能随环境与目标变化自适应。

Method: 借鉴世界模型范式：1) 编码器从真实行人视频轨迹预测中学习并冻结人类运动动力学表示；2) 行为者基于表示反复更新状态，生成“想象”的人群轨迹；3) 评论者为想象轨迹打分；4) 规划器结合评论者分数与用户代价选择动作；重复规划推进仿真，并可在运行时加入新代价（如避让/吸引）实现控制。

Result: 在不同到达条件下的人群生成与避让/吸引等运行时控制任务上进行大量评测；在大多数人群逼真度与碰撞指标上优于现有SOTA，并能在仿真过程中适配新目标。

Conclusion: Ctrl-CWM将世界模型式规划引入人群仿真，实现统一的生成与可控性；通过冻结动力学表示与代价可插拔的规划，在无需再训练的情况下实现对新目标的自适应并提升整体性能。

Abstract: Crowd simulation plays a central role in robot navigation, autonomous driving, and urban planning. For these applications, realistic simulation requires crowds to adapt their behavior to environmental changes and user objectives. However, existing methods that rely on predefined control settings have limited flexibility in accommodating new user-specified objectives. To address this limitation, we propose Ctrl-CWM, a multi-agent Controllable Crowd World Model that integrates crowd generation and run-time control. Our key idea is to adapt the world-model principle of planning using imagined futures to crowd simulation. To this end, Ctrl-CWM consists of an encoder that learns a representation of human motion dynamics, an actor that proposes pedestrian displacements, a critic that evaluates imagined crowd trajectories, and a planner that selects actions. We first learn human motion dynamics through trajectory prediction on real-world pedestrian videos and then freeze the encoder to preserve them. Using this representation, the actor generates imagined crowd trajectories through repeated state updates, and the planner combines the critic's scores with user costs to select actions. Repeated planning advances the simulated crowd, while additional user costs introduce new control objectives without retraining. We extensively evaluate crowd generation under varied agent arrival conditions and run-time control across avoidance and attraction scenarios. Ctrl-CWM outperforms the state-of-the-art method on most crowd realism and collision metrics, and adapts crowd behaviors to user-specified objectives introduced during simulation. The project page is available at https://jungyu0413.github.io/Ctrl-CWM

</details>


### [53] [InscriptionOCR: A Dataset and Method for Understanding Inscriptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09439)
*Jaidev Sanjay Khalane,Akbar Ali,V. N. Prabhakar,Shanmuganathan Raman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个端到端的AI流程处理阿育王时代的婆罗米文刻辞，从图像增强到OCR、转写及英译，并发布两个关键数据集以支撑研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 古代石刻图像退化严重且缺乏标准化数字资源，限制了自动化分析与历史研究；需要一个完整流程和公开数据来推动低资源文字的计算处理。

Method: 构建一条流水线：对石刻低质图像进行增强与修复；训练婆罗米文字OCR识别字符；将识别的婆罗米字符转写为罗马字；使用神经机器翻译将婆罗米语（普拉克里特）译为英语。同时发布两个数据集：约20万字符、约600类的InscriptionOCR数据集；约2000句对的普拉克里特-英语双语语料用于NMT。

Result: 实现了从原始石刻图到可读英文文本的自动处理能力，并提供了规模化的OCR与NMT训练数据集，为相关任务提供可用基线与资源。

Conclusion: 端到端框架与新数据集填补了婆罗米文计算处理的空白，为古文字识别、低资源OCR与数字碑铭学研究奠定基础，预期将促进后续方法与资源建设。

Abstract: Ancient script image restoration is a fundamental problem in computer vision, as it directly affects the reliable analysis and interpretation of historical documents and inscriptions. Ashokan Brahmi is an ancient script extensively used during the reign of Emperor Ashoka in the 3rd century BC, primarily for inscriptions in Prakrit. These inscriptions, including major and minor rock and pillar edicts, constitute a valuable yet largely unexplored source of data for computational analysis. The degraded nature of inscription imagery and the lack of standardized digital resources pose significant challenges for automated processing.
  We present an end-to-end AI-based framework for understanding ancient inscriptions that encompasses image enhancement, optical character recognition (OCR), transliteration, and neural machine translation (NMT). The proposed pipeline processes low-quality images captured directly from stone inscriptions, performs image restoration and Brahmi script character recognition, maps the recognized characters to the Roman script, and finally translates the resulting Prakrit text into English. We also introduce two new datasets: (i) InscriptionOCR Dataset: the largest publicly usable digital OCR dataset for Brahmi script to date, consisting of over 200,000 character images across about 600 classes, and (ii) a bilingual Prakrit-English parallel corpus comprising over 2,000 sentence pairs for NMT. We believe that the proposed framework and datasets will facilitate future research in ancient script analysis, low-resource OCR, and digital epigraphy.

</details>


### [54] [Mixture of Layers: Dynamic Layer Routing for Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09440)
*Jeonghwan Kim,Sofia Stoica,Jiwan Chung,Ansel Blume,Hyeonjeong Ha,Zhenhailong Wang,Xin Luna Dong,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Mixture of Layers (MoL)：在视觉补丁级别根据文本指令动态路由并稀疏聚合视觉编码器不同层的表示，以实现查询自适应的细粒度视觉理解；在多项细粒度任务上显著优于基线MLLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM多只用视觉编码器末层/倒数层或固定聚合，导致视觉抽象与查询无关，难以捕获小目标、空间细节、文字与细微属性等细粒度线索。需要一种能按查询选择不同层级表征的机制。

Method: 提出MoL：给定文本查询，对视觉编码器各层预测路由概率，并对选中的层执行top-k稀疏聚合。可在图像级、补丁级或混合级进行路由；按指令自适应地从中间层提取与任务相关的特征，接入MLLM进行推理。并分析各层感受野尺度与采样行为，解释层级采样的益处。

Result: 在7个细粒度视觉推理任务上显著提升：如V*整体准确率+18.9%，HRBench4K +4.5%，CharXiv +16.3%，且无需多分辨率输入、堆叠多视觉编码器或增加patch tokens数量。

Conclusion: 指令条件的层级路由能解锁视觉编码器不同层的互补信息，提供查询自适应的细粒度表征，显著增强MLLM的视觉感知与推理；条件化视觉表示是提升MLLM视觉能力的关键步骤。

Abstract: Pre-trained vision encoders contain layer-wise visual representations that differ in spatial granularity, semantic abstraction, and sensitivity to local details. However, most Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) rely on only the final or penultimate vision encoder representations or fixed aggregation rules, making visual abstraction largely query-agnostic and limiting access to fine-grained cues such as small objects, spatial details, text, and subtle visual attributes. In this work, we propose Mixture of Layers (MoL), an instruction-conditioned layer routing approach at the visual patch level that dynamically aggregates query-relevant latent representations from intermediate vision encoder layers. Given a text query, MoL predicts routing probabilities over vision encoder layers and performs a top-k sparse aggregation over selected hidden states at either the image level, patch level, or through a hybrid routing mechanism. In doing so, MoL enables query-adaptive access to layer-specific visual features for fine-grained visual reasoning. Our experiments across 7 fine-grained visual reasoning tasks demonstrate substantial performance improvements, especially across fine-grained visual grounding and understanding tasks such as +18.9% improvement on V* in overall accuracy, +4.5% on HRBench4K, and +16.3% on CharXiv compared to the baseline MLLMs, without resorting to multi-resolution inputs, simple interleaving of multiple vision encoders, or increasing the number of patch tokens. We study vision encoders' receptive field scales across different layers and their sampling behaviors to provide an in-depth analysis of why layer-wise sampling is helpful, demonstrating that conditional visual representations are a key step towards better visual perception and reasoning in MLLMs. Our project page is available at https://wjdghks950.github.io/mol.github.io/.

</details>


### [55] [TIRA: Tumor Immune Representation Adaptation for Zero-Shot Cross-Cancer MSI and TMB Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09441)
*Dasari Naga Raju*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TIRA框架：在无需目标域数据和测试时自适应的前提下，利用来源域的空间免疫拓扑来细化病理基础模型表示，从而提升MSI与TMB跨癌种零样本预测的稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于病理基础模型的MSI/TMB预测在跨癌种迁移时受形态学差异影响显著，且模型未显式利用可跨癌保持的免疫相关空间模式，导致泛化不足。

Method: 提出TIRA（Tumor Immune Representation Adaptation）：冻结基础模型的形态学特征，额外学习一个由拓扑监督的生物学（免疫）表示以刻画空间免疫拓扑；在tile级以该表示调制注意力权重，但汇聚时仅使用形态学特征；无需目标域数据，也不做测试时自适应；在TCGA-COAD+READ训练，零样本评估于CPTAC-COAD、TCGA-STAD、TCGA-UCEC、CPTAC-UCEC；在UNI2、CONCH、Virchow2基础模型上检验。

Result: 在UNI2上，跨癌种零样本到TCGA-STAD时，MSI AUROC由0.633提升至0.766，TMB AUROC由0.651提升至0.772；总体显示来源域的空间免疫拓扑可提升冻结病理基础模型表示的跨癌稳健性。

Conclusion: 利用来源域的空间免疫拓扑对冻结的基础模型表示进行条件化与适配，可在不访问目标域与不做测试时自适应的前提下，显著提升MSI与TMB的跨癌种零样本预测性能，验证了免疫空间模式作为跨癌共享信号的价值。

Abstract: Microsatellite instability-high (MSI-H) and high tumor mutational burden (TMB-H) are clinically relevant biomarkers, yet their histopathological prediction remains challenging when models are transferred across morphologically distinct cancer types. Immune-associated spatial patterns can persist across cancers despite these morphological differences, but foundation-model-based predictors trained on a single cancer do not explicitly use this information, limiting cross-cancer generalization. To address this limitation, we propose TIRA (Tumor Immune Representation Adaptation), a target-free framework that refines frozen foundation-model representations using spatial immune topology, without requiring target-domain data during model development or test-time adaptation. TIRA uses a topology-supervised biology representation to condition tile-level attention while pooling only morphological features for joint MSI and TMB prediction. We train TIRA on TCGA-COAD+READ and evaluate it zero-shot on CPTAC-COAD, TCGA-STAD, TCGA-UCEC, and CPTAC-UCEC, covering cross-site, cross-cancer, and combined cross-cancer-site distribution shifts under UNI2, CONCH, and Virchow2. With UNI2, TIRA improved zero-shot AUROC on TCGA-STAD from 0.633 to 0.766 for MSI and from 0.651 to 0.772 for TMB. Source-derived spatial immune topology improved the cross-cancer robustness of frozen pathology foundation-model representations.

</details>


### [56] [LighTROcc: Lightweight 4D Occupancy Forecasting via Instance-Centric 3D Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09444)
*Hwanhee Jung,SeungHyeon Kim,Inkyu Koo,Qixing Huang,Sang Ho Yoon,Sangpil Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LighTROcc：用少量学习到的实例查询直接从环视相机单次前向推理预测当前与未来3D占据，兼顾精度与效率，并保持实例时序一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4D占据预测多用稠密体素/BEV表示，分辨率和预测时长提升会导致计算成本暴涨；且缺乏显式实例身份，难以保持跨时间的一致性。需要一种更轻量、实例中心且可长期预测的表示与方法。

Method: 以实例为中心：用紧凑的学习查询表示可移动目标；通过注意力引导的前向lift（图像空间交叉注意+查询特定深度+相机几何）定位3D中心；每个实例用各向异性3D高斯混合表示其占据；通过预测位移在未来多步传播，实现连续且时序一致的占据预测；单次前向同时输出当前与未来占据。

Result: 在nuScenes与补充的nuScenes-Occupancy上，相比稠密和实例级基线，实例级预测精度更高，同时保持强的体素级占据质量；不同模型配置下在精度与效率之间取得更优平衡。

Conclusion: 紧凑的实例中心建模可有效用于基于相机的4D占据预测，LighTROcc以更低计算开销实现高精度且时序一致的预测，优于多种基线并具备良好可扩展性。

Abstract: Forecasting future 3D occupancy from surround-view cameras is essential for autonomous driving, yet existing approaches rely on dense voxel or bird's-eye-view representations whose cost grows rapidly with spatial resolution and prediction horizon. Because these representations do not explicitly maintain object identities, they also struggle to preserve instance consistency over time. We present LighTROcc, a lightweight instance-centric framework that represents movable objects with a compact set of learned queries and predicts present and future occupancy in a single forward pass. LighTROcc localizes each query through attention-guided forward lifting, combining image-space cross-attention, query-specific depth, and camera geometry to estimate its 3D center. Each instance is modeled as a mixture of anisotropic 3D Gaussians and propagated across future steps using predicted displacements, producing continuous, temporally consistent occupancy forecasts. Experiments on nuScenes and supplemented nuScenes-Occupancy show that LighTROcc outperforms the evaluated dense and instance-wise baselines in instance-level forecasting accuracy while maintaining strong voxel-level occupancy quality. Across different model configurations, LighTROcc achieves a favorable balance between forecasting accuracy and computational efficiency, demonstrating the potential of compact instance-centric modeling for camera-based 4D occupancy forecasting.

</details>


### [57] [From Global Alignment to Local Grounding: Zero-Shot Chinese Character Recognition with Radical Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09449)
*Yu-Heng Shih,Bing-Chen Wu,Tsz-To Wong,Ting-En Yen,Hong-Han Shuai,Bin-Hua Hsieh,Chien-An Chen,Yi-Ren Yeh,Ching-Chun Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个用于零样本中文字符识别的两阶段“全局到局部”框架：先用带空间先验的IDS做高召回全局检索，再用部首级验证精排；在ICDAR2013上达SOTA，Top-1为83.06%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CLIP式方法将字符的IDS与字形对齐，但仅用全局相似度，忽略部件的空间布局；且从已见类中隐式学习的布局难泛化到未见类。当不同字符只在细微部首上有差异时，全局匹配常把正确类排进前列却非第一。需要一种既保留全局召回、又能基于局部结构精细区分的方案。

Method: 两阶段框架：1) STG-CLIP：在IDS上显式注入树结构位置和部首级几何先验，构建具空间意识的原型，与字形对齐以进行高召回的全局检索；2) RVM（Radical Verification Module）：对每个候选，以其部首实例为查询，在输入字形中寻找空间兼容的区域进行匹配验证；当领先候选的全局分数接近时，通过基于边际的门控激活RVM以进行重排序。

Result: 在ICDAR2013字符级零样本设定下，使用2755个已见类达成83.06% Top-1精度，优于现有方法；消融显示显式几何先验与部首级验证均带来互补提升。

Conclusion: 通过在全局阶段引入空间先验并在局部阶段进行部首验证，该方法显著提升ZS-CCR的泛化与精排能力，尤其在细微部件差异场景下有效，达到SOTA表现。

Abstract: Zero-shot Chinese character recognition (ZS-CCR) aims to recognize characters whose categories are never observed during training, and typically relies on the compositional structure shared between seen and unseen characters. Recent CLIP-style methods represent this structure with the Ideographic Description Sequence (IDS) and align it with glyph images in a shared embedding space. However, they rely on a single global image--IDS similarity that discards the spatial layout of radicals and, being learned only implicitly from seen classes, generalizes poorly to unseen ones; moreover, global matching often retrieves the correct character within the top candidates yet fails to rank it first when characters differ only in subtle local radicals. To address these issues, we propose a global-to-local two-stage framework. In the first stage, STG-CLIP augments the IDS with explicit tree-position and radical-level geometric priors, yielding a spatial-aware prototype that provides a consistent spatial description across seen and unseen categories for high-recall global retrieval. In the second stage, the Radical Verification Module (RVM) uses the radical instances of each retrieved candidate as queries to verify whether the corresponding radicals can be matched to spatially compatible regions in the input glyph. A margin-based gating rule activates the RVM only when the leading global candidates receive similar similarity scores. Experiments on the ICDAR2013 benchmark demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance under the character-level zero-shot setting, obtaining 83.06% top-1 accuracy with 2,755 seen classes. Ablation studies further show that the explicit geometric priors and radical-level verification provide complementary improvements.

</details>


### [58] [Iris-3B: Going Beyond the Latent with Pixel-Space Diffusion Training, Conversion and Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09450)
*Hanqiu Li Cai,Chema Garabito*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 作者比较像素空间扩散/变换器模型与潜变量模型在细粒度下游任务上的优势，结果显示并无显著收益；但其像素空间模型Iris-3B在规模与文生图质量上达到了与主流潜变量模型相当的水平，并开放权重与代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社区常认为像素空间模型避免VAE量化损失，或许在需要细节的下游任务（深度估计、图像恢复/超分）更有优势。作者希望系统验证这一假设，并探索两条通往像素空间骨干的路径：从零训练大型像素模型与把潜变量模型转换为像素空间。

Method: 1) 训练Iris-3B：3B参数、像素空间文本到图像Transformer，使用PixelDiT的PiT头，分辨率课程256→512→1024；在256^2上先做目标与表征对齐的消融以决定扩展配置。2) 将预训练潜变量模型FLUX.2 Klein base 4B转换到像素空间。3) 在两个下游任务上微调并对齐配方：单目深度估计（直接回归）与图像恢复/4×超分（DIV2K）。4) 记录训练配方、失败模式与潜在混杂因素。

Result: - 在单目深度估计上：Iris-3B与潜变量版FLUX.2 Klein表现相当；转换为像素空间的FLUX.2 Klein落后。- 在4× DIV2K图像恢复上：两种像素模型均未超过潜变量FLUX.2 Klein微调，转换版略微更差。- 尽管下游无优势，Iris-3B在文生图质量上与潜变量模型竞争：在1024^2分辨率、OneIG官方评测下匹配Qwen-Image。

Conclusion: 像素空间生成先验并未在所测下游任务中带来显著性能提升；潜变量模型仍具竞争力。然而，像素空间预训练在规模与质量上可行（Iris-3B达3B参数、与SOTA相当）。作者开源模型与代码以促进后续像素空间生成研究。

Abstract: Pixel-space diffusion models avoid the lossy VAE of latent models, which suggests an advantage on downstream tasks where fine-grained detail matters. We test this claim along both routes to a pixel-space backbone. We pretrain Iris-3B, a 3B-parameter pixel-space text-to-image transformer, from scratch through a $256\to512\to1024$ curriculum, after first ablating the prediction target and representation alignment at $256^2$ to decide what to scale. We also convert a pretrained latent model, FLUX.2 Klein base 4B, to pixel space. We fine-tune both families for monocular depth estimation and for image restoration/super-resolution. We find no significant improvement from using a pixel-space generative prior. Fine-tuned for depth with one matched direct-regression recipe, Iris-3B is level with the latent FLUX.2 Klein and the converted pixel FLUX.2 Klein falls behind it, and on $4\times$ DIV2K restoration neither pixel model beats a latent FLUX.2 Klein fine-tune, the converted one trailing it slightly. We document the recipes, the failure modes and the remaining confounds behind this negative result. Nevertheless, Iris-3B shows that pixel-space pretraining with the pixel-transformer (PiT) head of PixelDiT scales to 3B parameters and to text-to-image quality competitive with latent models, matching Qwen-Image on OneIG under the official evaluators at $1024^2$. We release its weights and training code in the hope that they help pave the way for further work on pixel-space generation.

</details>


### [59] [RLHND: Video Foundation Models as Physically Grounded Hand Trackers for Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09455)
*Seungjun Moon,Subin Jeon,Sangwoo Kim,Hanbyul Joo,Jinwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RLHND提出从单目自视角视频同时估计手部姿态与触觉（接触与力），以提升基于人类视频的机器人策略学习的物理一致性与可用性。它将预训练的视频扩散模型Cosmos 3改造成确定性的片段级特征提取器，并结合形状条件、解剖可行关节角与LBS特征扩散，实现SOTA的姿态与触觉估计，并验证了对机器人重定向与实机任务的效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手部跟踪多基于裁剪帧、缺少对手部运动与手-物交互的先验，导致姿态不准且物理不一致；同时缺乏接触与力等物理线索，限制了人类视频在机器人策略学习中的价值。需要一个既能利用强视频先验、又能输出可用于控制的触觉与姿态的统一方法。

Method: 将预训练Cosmos 3视频扩散模型通过“clean-latent conditioning”改造成确定性片段级特征抽取；基于此搭建两条流：姿态流预测解剖可行的手部关节角，可选用形状参数实现同视频及跨视频的一致手形；触觉专家流在冻结姿态流后训练，预测手表面的稠密接触与力。采用基于线性蒙皮(LBS)的特征扩散，实现逐顶点特征而无需昂贵的逐顶点注意力。

Result: 在多种基准上，姿态估计达到SOTA；接触与力估计同样SOTA。方法在重定向与真实机器人实验中展示了对机器人学习的实用性。

Conclusion: 将视频扩散模型先验引入手部跟踪，并联合估计姿态与触觉，可显著提升准确性与物理一致性，弥补人类视频用于机器人学习中的关键缺口；RLHND在学术基准与实机中均验证有效，代码将开源。

Abstract: Recently, approaches that leverage human video datasets for robot policy training have become increasingly prevalent. However, most existing hand trackers regress pose from cropped frames with limited priors on hand motion and object interaction, resulting in inaccurate and physically inconsistent estimates. Moreover, the lack of physical cues, e.g., contact and force, limits the use of human videos for robot policy training. To this end, we propose RLHND, a video foundation model-based hand tracking model that jointly estimates hand pose and realistic tactile information from monocular egocentric videos. RLHND turns the pre-trained Cosmos 3 video diffusion backbone into a deterministic clip-level feature extractor via clean-latent conditioning, carrying its learned priors on hand motion and hand-object interaction into tracking. For pose estimation, RLHND (i) predicts hand poses with anatomically plausible joint angles and (ii) enables optional conditioning on the shape parameter to maintain consistent hand shape within the same video and even across videos recorded by the same actor. For tactile estimation, a separate tactile expert stream, trained with the pose stream frozen, predicts dense contact and force over the hand surface. We further adopt LBS-based feature spreading to enable vertex-wise feature extraction without costly per-vertex attention. RLHND achieves state-of-the-art performance across various benchmark datasets for pose estimation, while also achieving state-of-the-art performance in contact and force estimation. Moreover, we demonstrate the utility of RLHND for robot learning through retargeting results and real-world robot experiments. The code will be publicly available at https://seungjun-moon.github.io/rlhnd/.

</details>


### [60] [An Invariant Tangent-Angle Descriptor and a Band U-Net for 2D Fragment Adjacency Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09459)
*Guillaume Brouillette,Alain Goupil,Pierre-Olivier Parisé,Fadel Touré*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于切向角轮廓比较与Band U-Net的两阶段管线，用于2D碎片邻接预测；在合成与真实基准上显著优于先前Siamese图像窗方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于局部图像窗的Siamese评分需处理旋转与起点对齐问题，且终端分类器仅检测带状结构，无法直接输出共享弧段。作者希望获得天然旋转与起点不变的度量，同时在做出邻接判断时提取具体相邻弧段，提高精度与实用性。

Method: 保留两阶段管线但替换两处：1) 将局部相似度由图像窗匹配改为轮廓窗口的切向角（tangent-angle）剖面比较，先天对旋转与起点不敏感；实现为无训练的似然比或轻量1D卷积匹配器。由此得到的分数填充为邻接矩阵。2) 将末端ResNet检测反对角带改为Band U-Net，对邻接矩阵进行带状分割并同时输出配对分类与共享弧段位置。

Result: 在原论文的合成数据上，切向描述子在所有配置中与或优于图像窗方法；修正评估后，整管线准确率达98%（原方法93–95%）。在PairingNet基准上（仅用合成数据训练），AUC=0.93；在PairingNet的pair-search协议下，学习型描述子在真实集上的Recall@10=0.82，显著高于原文最佳模型的0.56。

Conclusion: 以切向角描述子与Band U-Net替代旧组件，可实现旋转与起点不变、同时定位共享弧段的邻接预测，带来显著性能提升与更可解释的输出，并具备从合成到真实的良好迁移能力。

Abstract: This paper addresses the prediction of adjacency between pairs of 2D fragments based on their contours. We improved the two-stage architecture proposed in Beaulac's thesis, in which a rotation-equivariant Siamese convolutional neural network scores pairs of local image windows along the two contours of two fragments. The scores are gathered in an adjacency matrix in which a ResNet detects the partial anti-diagonal band that reveals the adjacency of two fragments. In the current work, we keep the pipeline and replace the local score by a comparison of tangent-angle profiles of contour windows, making it, by construction, invariant to fragment rotation and agnostic to the selected contour-starting point. These adaptations may be either a training-free likelihood ratio or a small one-dimensional convolutional model trained on corresponding points. We also replaced the final classifier by a band U-Net that segments the band and classifies the pair, so that the shared arc is obtained along with the decision. In the synthetic data set of the original thesis, the tangent descriptor performs as well as or better than the image-window approach in all tested configurations. The proposed pipeline reaches an accuracy of 98%, vs 93% to 95% for the original approach once its evaluation is corrected. We tested our pipeline, with models trained only on synthetic data, on the PairingNet benchmark, and obtained an AUC of 0.93. Furthermore, under the PairingNet pair-searching protocol conditions, our learned descriptor obtains a Recall@10 of 0.82 on the real set against 0.56 from the best model of the original paper.

</details>


### [61] [InstanceBench: Diagnosing Referential Reasoning and Target Identity in Referring Expression Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09478)
*Yuchen Li,Shaoyang Zhou,Yiran Wang,Ruiyi Deng,Haoyu Wang,Ziru Wei,Zhen Zhao,Luping Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出InstanceBench用于诊断指代表达分割的实例级推理能力，提供目标一致性与逻辑敏感的度量，并显示主要瓶颈在于目标选择而非掩码生成；匹配式监督可提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RES评测难以区分不同指称逻辑、无法检验在多条有效指引路径下目标是否保持一致，也混淆了目标选择与掩码生成错误，导致对模型实例级指代推理能力的洞察不足。

Method: 构建InstanceBench：包含6194张图、9264个目标实例、25077条人工验证表达；以目标为中心的表达集合（TCES）固定同一图像与目标掩码，配对最小表达与使用不同线索/路径但同目标的变体。提出精炼的指称逻辑分类（直接证据、同类选择、关系/组合、排除），通过逻辑关键的表达构造抑制捷径。设计身份感知指标：衡量在阈值下的目标保持率与集合级成功率，并将目标选择错误与掩码生成错误分离。对22个原生掩码RES检查点进行评测，并做受控干预与受控训练子集实验。

Result: 最强模型在标准mIoU达67.1%，但在All@0.7身份感知指标仅59.6%；干预实验验证模型对语言的敏感性；错误分解显示主要瓶颈是目标选择而非掩码解码；在受控训练子集上，引入与指标匹配的监督可提升身份感知表现。

Conclusion: InstanceBench实现“度量-诊断-改进”闭环：衡量跨不同指引路径的目标一致性，定位失败源并评估定向干预。结果表明当前RES模型在实例级目标选择方面存在显著短板，且可通过匹配式监督得到改善。

Abstract: Referring Expression Segmentation (RES) links natural-language descriptions to pixel-level object masks. Yet standard evaluation provides limited insight into instance-level referential reasoning: it does not systematically distinguish referential logics, test target preservation across valid grounding paths, or separate target-selection from mask-generation errors. We introduce InstanceBench, an instance-centered diagnostic benchmark comprising 6,194 images, 9,264 target instances, and 25,077 human-verified expressions. Each target-centric expression set (TCES) fixes the image and target mask while pairing a minimal expression with a same-target variant that uses another valid cue or grounding path. A compact referential-logic taxonomy spans direct target evidence, same-class selection, relational and compositional grounding, and exclusion, while logic-critical construction suppresses simpler shortcuts. Identity-aware metrics measure target retention and set-level success while separating selection from mask-generation errors. Across 22 native-mask RES checkpoints from 18 model families, the strongest checkpoint reaches 67.1% mIoU but only 59.6% All@0.7. Controlled interventions confirm language sensitivity, while failure decomposition identifies target selection rather than mask decoding as the main bottleneck. On a controlled training subset, matched supervision improves identity-aware performance, showing that the diagnosed capability responds to targeted supervision. Collectively, InstanceBench supports a measure-diagnose-improve cycle: measuring target consistency across grounding paths, localizing failure sources, and evaluating targeted interventions.

</details>


### [62] [Gaussian Material Fields for Volumetric Multi-Energy CT Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09492)
*Jian Lin,Jiancheng Fang,Hongming Shan,Shaoyu Wang,Yang Chen,Qiegen Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出“高斯材料场”用于多能CT的体素材料分解：共享各向异性3D高斯几何基，材料系数独立非负；结合可微光谱前向模型与自适应密度控制，实现联合重建并显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多能CT需要在同一空间域内表示多种材料体分布，既共享结构又保留材料差异。现有体素/网格表示在表达局部细节与跨材料共享方面受限，难以兼顾精度、适应性与计算效率。

Method: 1) 表示：用共享的各向异性3D高斯原语构成连续空间基；为每种材料赋予独立的非负系数，不绑定跨材料的权重。2) 前向模型：构建可微光谱投影模型，将高斯沿射线的路径积分与已标定的基矩阵相结合，实现从材料场到多能投影的端到端可微映射。3) 优化：联合优化几何（高斯位置、尺度、方向、密度）与材料系数。4) 自适应：材料感知的自适应密度控制，在聚合前保持材料特定的细化证据，动态调节局部表示容量以同时捕获大范围成分与稀疏细节。5) 评估：用公开CT数据生成的伪参考材料图，合成多能投影进行重建与对比、消融、效率测量。

Result: 在15个案例中，相比最强基线：平均PSNR提升4.03 dB，SSIM提升4.96%，NRMSE降低33.45%；材料层面更好恢复局部结构；运行时间与内存呈有利的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 高斯材料场是适用于多材料体重建的显式、自适应表示；通过共享几何与独立材料系数以及可微光谱模型与密度自适应控制，实现更高准确度与更优计算性能，优于现有方法。

Abstract: Volumetric material decomposition in multi-energy computed tomography requires a representation that organizes multiple three-dimensional material fields in a common spatial domain while retaining differences in composition and local structure. We observe that spatial primitives can be shared across materials without tying their coefficients, but their local capacity must respond to material-specific reconstruction needs. We introduce Gaussian material fields, which represent multiple material distributions with shared anisotropic 3D Gaussian primitives and independent nonnegative material coefficients. The shared geometry defines a continuous spatial basis, while the coefficients determine each primitive's contribution to the individual material fields. To reconstruct this representation from multi-energy projections, a differentiable spectral forward model combines Gaussian material path integrals with a calibrated basis matrix, enabling joint optimization of spatial geometry and material composition. Material-aware adaptive density control retains material-specific refinement evidence before aggregation and adjusts local representation capacity to accommodate both spatially extensive components and sparse details. Experiments use synthesized multi-energy projections generated from pseudo-reference material maps constructed by conventional methods from publicly available CT data. Across 15 cases, our approach improves average PSNR by 4.03 dB and SSIM by 4.96% over the strongest baseline, while reducing NRMSE by 33.45%. Material-wise comparisons and component ablations support improved recovery of localized structures, while runtime and memory measurements show favorable computational scaling. These results establish Gaussian material fields as an explicit, adaptive representation for volumetric multi-material reconstruction.

</details>


### [63] [SpatialUQ: Post-Hoc Uncertainty Quantification from Spatial Consistency in Black-Box Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09498)
*Md Kawsher Mahbub,Milon Biswas,Mirza Niaz Morshed,Wei Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SpatialUQ：仅用模型输出概率、通过空间一致性评估不确定性的后验方法；在胸片分类任务上，计算高效、检测失败更准、且校准更好；对弥散型病变适用，但对小结节较弱；与模型质量正相关，强域移位下相关性消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床部署的视觉模型常为黑盒，推理时无法访问内部、重训或取得真值；现有不确定性估计（如MC-Dropout、集成）要么成本高、要么校准差，且黑盒环境下难以使用。需要一种只依赖输出、可后处理、计算高效且可靠的UQ方法。

Method: 空间一致性假设：可信预测在不同空间裁剪上应一致。固定五个空间裁剪，加上全局预测，共6次确定性前向。计算全局预测与五裁剪概率均值之间的Jensen–Shannon散度，得到多裁剪不确定性分数（MUS）。并与熵、置信度、L1等进行监督融合。

Result: 在NIH ChestX-ray14（DenseNet-121，N=25,596）上：MUS用于失败检测AUC=0.784，优于MC-Dropout的0.664（p<1e-6），计算量为其1/5；校准方面SCE=0.049，优于L1的0.127；与熵/置信度/L1监督融合AUC达0.832，超过5模型集成的0.813。MUS随模型质量提升而提升：在BiomedCLIP上AUC=0.899，与质量相关ρ=0.846；分布内仍相关ρ=0.523；在VinBigData严重移位下相关性几乎消失ρ=0.027。对弥散病变效果好，对小、局灶性病变（如结节）较差。

Conclusion: SpatialUQ/MUS在黑盒、仅输出概率的条件下，实现低成本、良好校准且强竞争力的失败检测；适合弥散模式、随模型质量可扩展，但在严重域移位和小目标上可靠性下降。公开代码与材料可复现。

Abstract: Clinical vision models are often deployed as frozen black boxes with no access to internals, retraining, or ground truth at inference time. We introduce \textbf{SpatialUQ}, a post-hoc uncertainty method using only output probabilities. It measures the Jensen-Shannon divergence between the global prediction and the mean of five fixed spatial crops in six deterministic forward passes. The premise is simple, trustworthy predictions are spatially consistent. On NIH ChestX-ray14 (DenseNet-121, $N{=}25{,}596$), our Multicrop Uncertainty Score (MUS) reaches $0.784$ failure-detection AUC versus $0.664$ for MC-Dropout ($p{<}10^{-6}$) at one-fifth the compute, with native calibration ($\text{SCE}{=}0.049$ vs.\ $0.127$ for $\ell_1$), the best-calibrated among methods above 0.78 AUC. A supervised fusion of MUS with entropy, confidence, and $\ell_1$ reaches $0.832$, outperforming a five-member ensemble ($0.813$). MUS scales with model quality, reaching $0.899$ with BiomedCLIP ($ρ= 0.846$), while this relationship remains meaningful in-distribution ($ρ= 0.523$) but breaks down under severe distribution shift (VinBigData, $ρ= 0.027$). MUS is well-suited to diffuse findings but is less dependable for small focal lesions such as nodules. Code and experimental materials are publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/kawsher11/SpatialUQ.

</details>


### [64] [RT-DETR-World: Transferring Rich LLM Semantics to Real-Time Open-Vocabulary Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09502)
*Yupeng Zhang,Ziyi Zhao,Juntao Cheng,Sheng Wang,Ningnan Guo,Ruize Han,Liang Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RT-DETR-World：在保持实时/轻量推理的同时，通过训练期描述监督与双路径对齐，提升开放词表检测的零样本泛化与效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时开放词表检测既要支持未见类别识别，又要满足严格延迟/算力约束；现有方法多聚焦词表覆盖与高效匹配，但小模型难以吸收实例语义与场景关系，限制零样本泛化。

Method: 1) 构建包含三层监督的GroundingCapv2：类别名、对象描述（实例语义）、图像描述（关系与场景）。这些描述仅在训练期使用。2) 提出双路径描述对齐DDA：部署一致的轻量MiniLM通道提供类名与对象描述对齐；训练期引入LLM教师通道，离线预计算教师特征，用于对象级和图像级的语义对齐与全局表征监督；教师侧模块训练后移除。3) 关系感知的负样本放松RNR：依据教师提供的语义相似度，对“语义相关的负样本”降低惩罚，保留对精确正样本的强监督。4) 基于紧凑DETR架构（RT-DETR）并保留轻量的查询-文本匹配以维持实时性。

Result: 在零样本检测上达到有竞争力的精度，并在精度-效率（速度/参数量）权衡上优于同类实时OVD方法。

Conclusion: 通过训练期丰富描述监督与教师引导，将实例与场景语义“蒸馏”进紧凑检测器，在不增加推理开销的前提下显著提升开放词表零样本能力；代码将开源。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary detection (OVD) recognizes categories unseen during training through textual category queries, yet achieving strong generalization with real-time efficiency remains challenging. Beyond vocabulary scaling, zero-shot generalization may benefit from reusable visual--semantic cues learned from seen data, including attributes, actions, states, and contextual relations. Existing real-time OVD methods primarily emphasize vocabulary coverage and efficient region/query--text matching; under strict efficiency constraints, compact detectors may struggle to absorb rich instance semantics and scene context. We propose RT-DETR-World, a compact DETR-style detector that transfers the rich semantics conveyed by descriptions during training while retaining lightweight query--text matching at inference. We construct GroundingCapv2 with three levels of supervision: category names for standard OVD, object descriptions conveying instance-level semantics, and image descriptions conveying object relations and scene context. These descriptions serve only as training-time semantic supervision. To help the compact detector absorb these semantics, we propose Dual-Path Description Alignment (DDA), combining a deployment-consistent MiniLM pathway with a training-only LLM teacher. MiniLM provides query--category supervision and object-description alignment, while offline teacher features supervise matched queries and global visual representations at the object and image levels, respectively. All teacher features are precomputed, and the teacher-side modules are removed after training. We further propose Relation-Aware Negative Relaxation (RNR), which uses teacher-derived semantic similarities to relax related negatives while preserving exact positives. Experiments demonstrate competitive zero-shot accuracy and a favorable accuracy--efficiency trade-off. The code will be released.

</details>


### [65] [TERRA: Learning Transportable Latent Actions through Temporal Effect Representation and Relational Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09509)
*Tianxingjian Ding,Mubarak Shah,Yu Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TERRA与EAT：用“时间效应”表征视觉转移动作，并在跨初始状态重用时用效应锚定对齐，从而学到可复用的潜在动作；在LIBERO等基准上优于或匹配现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在动作能弱监督机器人策略，但存在两大难题：1) 用什么信息表征转移（仅终点差异会丢失运动过程，完整序列又含干扰）；2) 同一潜在码在不同起始状态是否语义一致（仅靠重建无法约束）。作者希望在同一框架内既压缩有效的时序信息，又确保跨情境复用的一致性。

Method: TERRA以“时间效应”表征转移：由净特征变化（起止差）+短窗低阶动态分量组成，学习连续潜在码；并以该效应空间为跨情境参照。EAT（Effect-Anchored Transport）在新初始状态解码潜在码，并将得到的效应锚定到源效应，使潜在码由其跨情境的“所做之事”来定义而非仅源转移。评估采用冻结线性读出器。

Result: 在动作预测上优于UniVLA与LAPA式基线；在视觉干扰下退化更慢；跨情境迁移时更忠实于供体动作，且同等训练预算的对照表明主要增益来自EAT；在匹配预训练规模下，LIBERO平均成功率93.4%，优于UniVLA的91.8%。

Conclusion: 通过时间效应表征与效应锚定迁移，潜在动作获得稳定且可复用的语义，带来更强的鲁棒性与迁移性；EAT贡献显著。整体系统在标准基准上实现小幅但稳定的SOTA级提升。

Abstract: Latent actions supervise robot policies with action-like codes inferred from visual transitions, and their usefulness hinges on two questions: what a code keeps from a transition, and whether it still means the same thing when reused in a different initial state. The first is a tension in time: an endpoint difference discards how motion unfolds, while the full sequence admits nuisance variation. The second is left open by reconstruction, which only ever observes a latent together with the state it came from. We argue that both questions can be answered in the same place. TERRA (Temporal Effect Representation and Relational Alignment) describes a transition by a compact temporal effect, its net feature change together with a low-order within-window dynamics component, and learns a continuous latent from this effect. The same effect space then serves as the reference for reuse: Effect-Anchored Transport (EAT) decodes a latent in other initial states and anchors the resulting effect to the one observed at its source, so that the latent is shaped by what it does across contexts rather than only by the transition it came from. With frozen linear readers, TERRA predicts actions more accurately than UniVLA and a LAPA-style baseline, degrades more slowly under visual distractors, and keeps transported transitions faithful to the donor action as the recipient context moves farther away; a same-budget control shows that these gains come largely from EAT. At matched pretraining scale, the complete system reaches 93.4% average success on LIBERO, compared with 91.8% for UniVLA.

</details>


### [66] [LiG-DETR: Local-in-Global Reassembly in Latent Space for Aerial Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09511)
*Yupeng Zhang,Fangzhuo Gao,Juntao Cheng,Ziyi Zhao,Liang Wan,Ruize Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LiG-DETR：在统一DETR框架内通过全局-局部重组直接获取并融合高保真局部特征，显著提升航拍小中目标检测，同时兼顾效率与泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航拍图像目标尺度跨度大、密度高：小目标在下采样/压缩中信息易丢失，而中大目标需要全局语境。现有两类方案各有限：图像切片虽有清晰局部证据但需独立预测与后处理；基于全图特征/查询的优化保持端到端但在已压缩表示上操作，难以恢复细粒度信息。亟需一种能在特征退化前直接获取高保真局部证据、且与全局信息端到端融合的方法。

Method: 将“切片”重释为“高保真局部特征采集”，构建Efficient Global-Local Reassembly：共享编码器并行提取全局特征与局部放大特征；将局部特征投影到检测器特征空间并按原始空间位置重组为与全局对齐的局部特征层；使用单个DETR解码器联合解码全局+局部特征。为控算力：提出Context-Preserved Selective Reassembly，在保持密集布局的同时仅对信息丰富区域进行高分辨率编码；提出Density-Aware Adaptive Query Allocation，依据编码器proposal分数自适应分配解码查询预算。

Result: 在小/中目标上取得显著提升，同时保持大目标性能；在精度-效率上具备更优折中，并在跨域泛化方面表现更好。

Conclusion: LiG-DETR证明：在端到端DETR中引入全局-局部特征重组、于退化前获取并融合高保真局部证据，可同时提升小目标检测与整体效率/泛化。代码将开源。

Abstract: Aerial object detection faces substantial scale and density variations. Small objects are easily degraded by downsampling and feature compression, while medium and large objects require sufficient global context. Existing methods mainly follow two paradigms: image slicing provides clearer local evidence but relies on independent crop-level prediction and post-processing, whereas feature- and query-level optimization preserves unified inference but operates on already compressed full-image representations, limiting recovery of fine-grained information. This raises a key question: can aerial detection directly acquire high-fidelity local evidence before feature degradation and integrate it into a unified end-to-end framework? To this end, we propose LiG-DETR, an Efficient Global-Local Reassembly framework that reformulates image slicing as high-fidelity local feature acquisition. A shared encoder extracts global and locally magnified features, which are projected into the detector feature space. The projected local features are reassembled according to their original spatial locations to form a globally aligned local feature level, and a single DETR decoder jointly decodes global and local features. To reduce redundant computation, Context-Preserved Selective Reassembly focuses high-resolution encoding on informative regions while preserving a dense feature layout, and Density-Aware Adaptive Query Allocation adapts the decoder query budget using encoder proposal scores. Experiments show substantial gains on small and medium objects while retaining strong large-object performance, with favorable accuracy--efficiency trade-offs and improved cross-domain generalization. The code will be released.

</details>


### [67] [OmniCam: Omni-Camera Trajectory Generation via Geometry-Grounded Pose Token Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09513)
*Zhenyang Liu,Chenjie Cao,Yisu Zhang,Xuhui Zuo,Xiangyang Xue,Yanwei Fu,Tengfei Wang,Chunchao Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OmniCam：从单幅全景图与文本描述自回归生成相机位姿序列；并构建OmniCaT数据集。通过几何约束的姿态token学习与目标感知条件，提高轨迹精度并显著减少碰撞，优于重训GenDoP及其他基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言驱动相机运动需同时考虑场景几何与目标构图（framing）。现有方法难以从单张全景图理解完整几何并生成目标感知、无碰撞的连贯轨迹，且缺乏大规模针对性数据集与稳定的姿态表示。

Method: OmniCam为自回归序列生成模型：1) 全景点云编码器获取全向几何上下文；2) 采用“绝对旋转+相对平移”的混合token化，并保持四元数符号时序一致；3) 几何与语义分离的条件通道，并显式引入3D目标锚点以进行目标感知构图。构建OmniCaT数据集（26.77万条，四类相机行为）用于训练与评测。

Result: 在OmniCaT评测中，相较在同数据集重训的GenDoP，OmniCam将轨迹误差降低28–47%，碰撞率降低65.8%；对各指标最强基线，ATE与碰撞率分别降低43.0%与62.3%。消融验证几何与目标感知条件的有效性；下游实验展示在相机控制视频生成与机器人主动感知上的收益。

Conclusion: 几何对齐的姿态token学习与目标感知条件可从单幅全景与文本稳健生成无碰撞、高精度相机轨迹；OmniCam和OmniCaT为语言到相机运动的研究与应用提供了有效方法与基准。

Abstract: Camera trajectories control viewpoint changes in video generation, scene reconstruction, and robotic perception. Generating them from language requires both scene geometry and target-aware framing. We introduce OmniCam, an autoregressive model that generates camera pose sequences from a single panorama and textual trajectory descriptions. Its geometry-grounded pose token learning combines three components: a panoramic point-cloud encoder for omnidirectional geometric context; hybrid absolute-rotation and relative-translation tokenization with temporally consistent quaternion signs; and separate geometric and semantic conditioning streams with an explicit 3D target anchor. We also construct OmniCaT, containing 267,700 trajectories across four camera behaviors. On the reported OmniCaT evaluation, OmniCam reduces trajectory errors by 28--47% and collision rate by 65.8% relative to GenDoP retrained on OmniCaT. Against the best baseline for each metric, the ATE and collision reductions are 43.0% and 62.3%, respectively. Component ablations support the use of geometric and target-aware conditioning, while downstream experiments examine camera-controlled video generation and robotic active perception.

</details>


### [68] [STRIKE: Learning Visual State Transitions for Physical World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09514)
*Wenbin Teng,Tianshuo Xu,Depu Meng,Yuelei Li,Quentin Herau,Yihan Hu,Yajie Zhao,Wei Zhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: STRIKE 将“视觉状态转移”与“稠密视频生成”解耦：先学会从当前图像+局部转移动作+时间推断下一状态，再用生成模型补全整段视频，从而更好地建模物理世界的因果与一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型往往只追求连贯画面，难以准确反映物理交互导致的场景变化与因果约束；缺乏能表达“状态—动作—时间—结果”的中间表示。

Method: 1) 从训练视频中抽取事件对齐的观测状态，并与转移描述和时间偏移配对，构造监督；2) 训练图像级转移模型：输入当前图像、局部转移说明、已过时间，预测下一场景配置；3) 推理时用预训练视觉-语言规划器产生时间化的转移说明，递归应用转移模型得到未来状态序列；4) 另行训练的动态生成模型据此状态序列与时间戳生成完整视频。

Result: 在 Physics-IQ Verified、PhyGenBench、Pisa-Experiments、RoboTwin2.0 上，相比相同视频骨干的基线，STRIKE 在物理一致性与操控视频保真度等基准指标上显著提升。

Conclusion: 将“可学习的视觉状态转移”作为中间表示，可提升物理世界建模的可控性与一致性；解耦规划、状态转移与视频生成是有效范式。

Abstract: Physical world modeling requires predicting how interactions change a scene, not merely generating coherent motion. We propose STRIKE, a framework that separates visual state transition learning from dense video generation. We construct event-aligned supervision by extracting observed states from training videos and pairing them with transition descriptions and temporal offsets. An image-based transition model learns to predict the next scene configuration from the current image, a local transition specification, and elapsed time. At inference, a pretrained vision-language planner predicts time transition specifications, and recursive application of the learned transition model produces a sequence of future visual states.
