We propose DeepTopoClustering (DTC), an unsupervised framework for deriving a hierarchical process taxonomy from object-based surface activities, so-called 4D objects-by-change (4D-OBCs). We transform each 4D-OBC into a GeoMorphogram, a distributional sequence representing the temporal evolution of topographic change within a spatially bounded surface activity. A convolutional autoencoder learns latent embeddings from GeoMorphograms, which are jointly optimized using a hierarchical deep clustering objective to organize surface activities into a hierarchy. We evaluate the learned hierarchy using expert annotations on two 4D datasets of sandy beach sites and their combination. DTC with GeoMorphograms achieves the highest agreement with expert judgment at the taxonomy level comprising eight major process types ($F_1=0.78$, match accuracy $=0.92$), outperforming dimensionality reduction and conventional flat clustering. The learned taxonomy separates major erosion- and deposition-dominated activities and distinguishes finer subtypes based on change magnitude, duration, compactness, and temporal evolution. DTC thus provides a scalable and interpretable route from 4D change detection to a data-driven, expert-supported surface process taxonomy, advancing automated knowledge derivation for understanding surface dynamics in topographic monitoring.

</details>


### [103] [Global Average Precision for Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09863)
*Bill Psomas,Mohammad Mahdi,Michalis Thomas,Danda Pani Paudel,Giorgos Tolias,Giorgos Kordopatis-Zilos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种全局平均精度的可微替代损失gSAP，统一在批内对所有查询-候选对进行排序与优化，相比按查询优化的mAP代理或InfoNCE，在低温度下仍可训练，并提升跨任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索指标与损失（mAP、InfoNCE、每查询AP代理）按单查询比较相似度，忽略跨查询的相似度可比性；而实际系统常需单一阈值做全局判别，需要跨查询一致的相似度刻度。gAP可度量这种全局一致性，但不可微，亟需可微代理以端到端训练。

Method: 提出gSAP（global surrogate AP）：以整个相似度矩阵与二值正负矩阵为输入，在批层面联合考虑所有查询-候选对，构造对gAP的可微排序代理损失；与编码器、模态、监督来源无关，可作为InfoNCE或AP代理的即插即用替代；在低温度（sharp分布）下仍保持有效梯度。

Result: 将gSAP替换进既有训练范式，在监督度量学习、跨模态对齐与自监督预训练中均取得更好性能；在相同精度下，比最强AP代理可多检索出至多4倍的正样对；当加入无正样查询时退化最小；相似度在不同查询间更一致，支持单一全局阈值；学到的表征在迁移、kNN与零样本分类上更优。

Conclusion: 优化全局排序的一致性是关键；gSAP提供了简洁、可微、稳健的全局AP代理，既能在低温下训练，又能普适替换现有按查询损失，带来检索与下游表征学习的全面提升。

Abstract: Standard information retrieval metrics, such as mean Average Precision (mAP), assess performance one query at a time, based on how the similarities between a query and its positives compare against those with its negatives. The same holds for common representation learning losses, such as InfoNCE and per-query AP surrogates. None of them considers whether similarities are comparable across queries, which any system with a single decision threshold relies on. Global Average Precision (gAP) does, by ranking all query-candidate pairs in one list and computing a single AP. We introduce gSAP, a differentiable surrogate of gAP. It needs only a similarity matrix and a binary matrix marking the positive pairs, the same input as existing losses, so it is a drop-in replacement for them and agnostic to the encoder, the modality, and the source of supervision. Since it considers all possible pairwise comparisons in the batch jointly, it also remains trainable at low temperatures, a regime where per-query surrogates run out of gradient. Swapping it into established recipes improves supervised metric learning, cross-modal alignment, and self-supervised pretraining, where, to our knowledge, it is the first ranking loss to replace the community standard InfoNCE in the latter two. Its similarities are more consistent across queries, which drives the gains under a universal threshold. gSAP retrieves up to four times as many positive pairs as the strongest AP surrogate at the same precision, and it degrades the least when queries with no positives in the database are added. Beyond thresholding, models trained with gSAP also learn better representations, with higher transfer, $k$NN and zero-shot classification accuracy.

</details>


### [104] [Bringing BNNs to Fast Event Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09873)
*Paul Longour,Julien Moreau,Franck Davoine*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文将二值神经网络与事件相机结合，通过提出适配的二值架构与PBEV表示，实现事件数据的高效分类；并表明利用RGB跨模态预训练可显著提升BNN在神经形态数据集上的精度，在N-Caltech101上以更少算力达到90.58%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BNN在资源受限设备上高效，但多用于传统帧式图像；事件相机同样主打高效（低时延、高动态范围、低功耗），然而二者结合研究稀缺。动机是探索如何让现代深度BNN直接处理事件数据，并在保持极高效率的同时获得有竞争力的识别精度。

Method: 1) 适配与评估现代深度BNN在事件数据上的表现；2) 提出Polar-wise Binary Event Volume（PBEV），把事件流编码为可由BNN直接处理的二值体素表示；3) 利用来自RGB数据的跨模态预训练，再在神经形态数据集上微调；4) 在N-Caltech101等基准上系统评测算力与精度。

Result: 在N-Caltech101分类任务上，最佳BNN达到90.58%准确率，同时相较全精度模型运算量减少约7.5倍；跨模态（RGB→事件）预训练带来精度提升；PBEV使事件数据端到端地被BNN以二值形式处理成为可能。

Conclusion: 二值化与事件视觉可有效结合：通过PBEV与跨模态预训练，BNN能在保持显著计算/存储优势的同时获得接近全精度的性能，为全二值化事件视觉系统迈出关键一步。

Abstract: Binary Neural Networks (BNNs) enable efficient deep learning deployment on resource constrained devices with weights and activations compressed to one bit, substantially reducing model size and inference cost. Event cameras offer complementary advantages, including low latency, high dynamic range, and low power consumption, by capturing asynchronous streams of events rather than dense image frames. Despite their shared emphasis on efficiency, the combination of these technologies remains largely unexplored. This work aims at adapting and evaluating modern deep BNN architectures on event data. We also show that cross-modal pretraining from RGB data can improve the classification accuracy of BNNs on neuromorphic datasets. We introduce the Polar-wise Binary Event Volume (PBEV), a binary representation that enables event-camera data to be processed directly by BNNs and represents a step toward fully binarized event-based vision systems. Best evaluated BNN on N-Caltech101 classification benchmarks shows 90.58% accuracy with 7.5x less operations than their full-precision counterparts.

</details>


### [105] [SANet: Selective Attention Network for Infrared Small Target Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09875)
*Yingmei Zhang,Wangtao Bao,Qin Xiao,Yong Yang,Weiguo Wan,Yitao Luo,Xueting Zou,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SANet用于红外小目标检测，通过双路径语义感知与选择性注意融合，在三大数据集上显著提升IoU与降低误警。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外小目标体积极小、对比度弱、背景复杂，易误检漏检，现有方法难以在检测精度与误警率间取得平衡，且U-Net静态跳连在跨尺度信息融合上存在局限。

Method: 构建选择性注意网络SANet：1）双路径语义感知模块并行使用标准卷积与风车（pinwheel）卷积，既保留局部空间一致性又获取更广上下文；2）空间与通道注意联合增强，提升目标-背景判别性；3）选择性注意融合模块以空间可变权重自适应整合多尺度特征，替代U-Net静态跳连，选择性强化显著区域并抑制伪目标。

Result: 在NUAA-SIRST、IRSTD-1K、NUDT-SIRST三基准上，SANet在IoU、归一化IoU、探测概率与误警率上取得竞争性结果；IoU比分别超出第二名1.93、4.32、2.21个百分点。

Conclusion: SANet有效提升微弱目标感知与判别特征表达，抑制复杂背景下伪警；选择性注意的跨尺度融合优于静态跳连，具备在海事监控与搜救等应用中的潜力。

Abstract: Infrared small target detection aims to accurately identify and locate dim targets in complex backgrounds and supports applications such as maritime surveillance and military search and rescue. However, the small size and weak contrast of infrared targets make it difficult to balance detection accuracy and false alarms. This paper proposes a selective attention network (SANet) for infrared small target detection. A dual-path semantic-aware module combines standard and pinwheel-shaped convolutions to preserve local spatial consistency and capture broader contextual information. Spatial and channel attention further refine the features and improve target-background discrimination. To address the limitations of static skip connections in U-Net, a selective attention fusion module adaptively integrates features across scales using spatially varying weights. It selectively enhances salient regions and improves discrimination between true targets and false alarms. Experiments on three public benchmarks, NUAA-SIRST, IRSTD-1K, and NUDT-SIRST, show that SANet achieves competitive performance in intersection over union (IoU), normalized IoU, detection probability, and false alarm rate. Its IoU exceeds that of the second-best method by 1.93, 4.32, and 2.21 percentage points, respectively. These results support the effectiveness of SANet in dim-target perception, discriminative feature representation, and background suppression.

</details>


### [106] [FedSSMCoOp: SSM Encoders for light-weight Federated Prompt Learning for Few-shot Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09907)
*Ankita Das,Ambarish Parthasarathy,Sumohana S. Channappayya,C. Krishna Mohan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FedSSMCoOp：在联邦学习下进行少样本多模态（图文）生物医学图像分类的新框架，利用SSM驱动的Vision Mamba与Cross Mamba实现跨模态交互，仅优化软提示与通信提示，无需LLM对齐，隐私友好、算力友好，较基线更稳且平均更轻量（约1.96×）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM多以余弦相似度对齐，忽略token级结构与早期跨模态交互；在生物医学联邦场景中，数据不可共享、标注稀缺且站点间统计异质性强，致使现有方法难以稳定泛化且计算昂贵。需要一种能在隐私约束下实现有效跨模态融合、少样本鲁棒、通信与计算高效的方案。

Method: 提出FedSSMCoOp联邦少样本分类框架：1) 模态侧采用SSM-based Vision Mamba与Cross Mamba块，实现高效序列建模与跨模态交互；2) 通过CoOp式软提示（soft-prompt）与专门的通信提示（communication-prompt）在联邦优化，仅更新少量可学习参数以降本提效；3) 端到端多模态训练，不依赖外部LLM进行特征对齐；4) 在多种生物医学图像数据集上进行联邦训练与评估。

Result: 相较基线，框架在多数据集与联邦异质性条件下表现更稳定；模型平均参数与/或计算开销约为基线的1/1.96（即更轻量1.96倍）。

Conclusion: FedSSMCoOp在联邦少样本生物医学图像分类中实现了隐私保护、跨模态有效对齐与高效训练，无需LLM且仅更新提示参数，在保持或提升性能的同时显著减轻模型/通信负担，具有实际部署潜力；代码将很快开源。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have shown strong performance across a wide range of downstream vision tasks, thanks to the complementary information contained in the respective domains. Despite the performance gains, most of these approaches rely on aligning these domains using the cosine similarity metric, which fails to capture token-level structure and cross-modal interactions prior to the classification stage. This is especially critical in biomedical applications under federated constraints, where data sharing is restricted, labeled data is scarce at each site, and it differs widely across institutions, leading to substantial statistical heterogeneity. To overcome this issue, we propose FedSSMCoOp, a federated few-shot image classification framework that enables multimodal learning while preserving data privacy. With the help of the SSM-based Vision Mamba and Cross Mamba blocks, and by optimizing only the soft-prompt and communication-prompt updates in the federated setting, the framework prioritizes both computation and performance. Importantly, this eliminates the need to use an external Large Language Model (LLM) for feature alignment. The framework is further trained and evaluated on various biomedical image datasets, and its performance is assessed. The proposed framework delivers stable performance relative to the baselines and is, on average, 1.96 times lighter. The corresponding script will be made available soon.

</details>


### [107] [Do Generative Priors Align with Human Naturalness Perception?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09928)
*Taiki Fukiage*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文探测视觉生成模型的“原生先验”是否与人类对自然性的规律一致，通过成对、内容保持的干预来比较模型的原生损失方向差异，发现其能复现塔切尔效应与对光照不一致的形状依赖敏感性，并与人类自然性判断高度相关；同时揭示人类一致性与总体敏感度在去噪进程中解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉生成模型学习自然图像分布，但不清楚其内在先验是否反映人类对“自然/不自然”感知的规律。现有单幅图像损失受内容与复杂度主导，难以作为感知自然性的探针；需要一种能隔离“规律违背”信号、并与人类判断可比的指标。

Method: 收集25个开放的图像与视频生成器，直接利用其“原生预测误差/损失”作为先验探针。设计成对、内容保持（paired, relational）的干预：选择性破坏人脸配置（如塔切尔化）或场景物理一致性（光照与形状关系），同时尽量控制低级统计不变。比较同一对中两图的方向性损失差（而非原始绝对损失），并与人类自然性判断、冻结视觉编码器特征距离、标准图像质量指标进行对照与控制。分析不同扩散去噪日程阶段的敏感度与人类一致性。

Result: 方向性损失差复现人类的选择性敏感与宽容：在人脸上重现经典塔切尔效应；在物理场景中表现出对光照不一致的形状依赖响应。对配对刺激的人类自然性评分预测显著（相关在人脸r≈0.84，物理场景r≈0.64），且在控制视觉特征距离与常规IQM后仍保留独特、与人类对齐的信号。总体对违规的敏感度与与人类一致性总体相关，但沿扩散去噪时间表系统性解耦：一致性在更早阶段达峰，而敏感度更晚增强。

Conclusion: 生成模型通过学习视觉分布，形成的损失景观内含与人类自然性感知相契合的结构；人类式“自然性”判断与泛化的违规检测并不等同，并在扩散过程阶段性分离。方向性损失差为评估模型人类对齐与感知规律捕获的有效工具。

Abstract: Visual generative models are trained to capture the probability distributions of natural images, yet whether their native priors reflect the regularities governing human perception of image naturalness remains an open question. Here, we probe these priors through native prediction errors across 25 open image and video generators. Because raw single-image losses are dominated by scene content and visual complexity, we evaluate directional loss differences using content-preserving, paired relational interventions that selectively disrupt facial configurations or physical illumination consistency while limiting changes in low-level image statistics. Across both domains, these loss differences reproduce human-like selective sensitivities and tolerances, capturing the classic Thatcher effect on faces and shape-dependent responses to illumination inconsistencies. Notably, these loss differences reliably track continuous gradations of human naturalness judgments across individual stimulus pairs (peaking at $r = .84$ on faces and $.64$ on physical scenes) and retain unique human-aligned signals even after controlling for feature distances from frozen vision encoders and standard image quality metrics. We also find that while overall sensitivity to these violations broadly covaries with human alignment across models, the two systematically decouple along denoising schedules, with alignment peaking earlier than sensitivity, revealing that human-like naturalness judgments dissociate from generic violation detection. Together, these findings demonstrate that learning visual distributions yields generative loss landscapes that capture distinct aspects of human naturalness perception.

</details>


### [108] [Purifying Backdoored Large Vision-Language Models by Removing Hijacked Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09941)
*Bojun Yang,Haochen Zhou,Zhifang Zhang,Haobo Wang,Songze Li,Lei Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OrthoPurify：用一次正交投影净化被后门污染的LVLM权重，近乎零开销、保持性能、攻成率接近零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM在安全关键场景中易受后门攻击，现有防御代价高：要么大量干净数据重新训练，要么推理时逐查询干预，影响效率与部署。需要一种无需重训、无推理开销、仍能有效去除后门的权重净化方法。

Method: 基于对后门训练的结构性分析，提出“方向劫持”现象：后门通过将少数权重更新方向从任务适配转向编码后门捷径来实现。为识别被劫持方向，构造伪良性参考模型：仅用少量干净样本在预训练权重上进行少步微调，因主导更新方向在前几步已稳定。随后以该参考的“良性方向”与可疑模型的更新做对比，用一次正交投影从后者的权重更新中去除与后门相关的方向分量，实现权重净化。

Result: 在多种基准与后门设置下，OrthoPurify将攻击成功率降至近零，同时几乎不损伤原始任务性能；不需要对受污染模型再训练，也不引入推理时开销。

Conclusion: 一次性正交投影即可高效去除LVLM权重中的后门方向，兼顾安全与性能，提供实用的无重训、低成本防御方案；代码已开源。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) are increasingly deployed in safety-critical applications, yet they remain vulnerable to backdoor attacks. Defending against such attacks remains costly, as existing methods require either extensive retraining on clean data or per-query intervention at inference time. To address this limitation, we propose OrthoPurify, a more efficient method to purify backdoored model weights via one-step orthogonal projection. Specifically, through structural analysis of backdoor weight updates, we find that the backdoor is encoded by diverting a small number of weight update directions from task adaptation to backdoor shortcut encoding, a phenomenon we term direction hijacking. However, identifying these hijacked directions requires a benign reference model, which is typically inaccessible to the defender. We show that a pseudo-benign model, obtained by fine-tuning the pretrained weights on only a small set of clean samples, provides a sufficient approximation, as the dominant update directions stabilize within the first few gradient steps. OrthoPurify uses this pseudo-benign reference to isolate the hijacked directions and removes them through a single projection on the weight update. Extensive experiments show that OrthoPurify reduces the attack success rate to near zero while preserving the original performance across diverse benchmarks, without retraining the backdoored model or introducing inference-time overhead. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/womeimingzi/OrthoPurify.

</details>


### [109] [MSU Team at the Explainable Deepfake Detection Challenge 2026: Grounded Artifact Evidence for Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09952)
*Artem Filippov,Aleksandr Gushchin,Kirill Koltsov,Dmitriy Vatolin,Anastasia Antsiferova*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个模块化的可解释深伪检测系统：多主干视觉检测器+伪证据掩膜监督+对比学习分离伪迹/真实性证据+类条件多模态大模型生成解释与简化。测试集上准确率0.9349、解释分数0.5571、总分0.7456。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成图像日益逼真，传统深伪检测不仅要准确还需可解释、能定位可视取证线索。挑战赛要求同时输出真假判断及“复杂/简洁”两级、可视证据支撑的解释。

Method: 1) 检测器：多主干融合，结合多个DINOv3与Mesorch操控定位特征，整合预训练视觉表征、DCT感知线索与多尺度取证信息。2) 证据注入：基于Grounding-DINO的伪掩膜流程，把训练解释中的局部伪迹描述转成弱监督patch级监督，学习Artifact Evidence Map。3) 特征分离：设计局部patch级对比目标，无需成对图或像素级掩膜，将“伪迹证据”与“真实性证据”在特征空间拉开。4) 语言生成：类条件Qwen3-VL分别为假/真生成复杂解释，再用经GRPO优化的文本简化模型生成简洁解释。5) 在XPlainVerse挑战子集训练与评测。

Result: 在XPlainVerse完整测试集上，检测准确率0.9349，解释得分0.5571，最终挑战分0.7456。

Conclusion: 模块化设计有效兼顾检测精度与可解释性；弱监督伪掩膜与patch级对比学习能在缺乏精细标注时分离并对齐证据；类条件多模态与简化模块实现面向用户的多层次解释。在挑战赛上取得较高综合成绩。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative image models have made many manipulated images highly realistic, raising the need for detectors that are not only accurate but also able to provide visual evidence for their decisions. In this paper, we present our solution to the Explainable Deepfake Detection Challenge [2] on the XPlainVerse dataset [1], where systems are required to predict whether an image is real or fake and generate both complex and simple explanations grounded in visible forensic cues. Our method follows a modular detection-and-explanation design. For the real/fake decision, we build a multi-backbone detector that combines several DINOv3 models with Mesorch manipulation-localization features, bringing together pretrained visual representations, DCT-aware cues, and multi-scale forensic information. To inject explanation evidence into the detector, we use a Grounding-DINO-based pseudo-mask generation pipeline that converts local artifact descriptions from training explanations into weak patch- level supervision for an Artifact Evidence Map. We further introduce a local patch-level contrastive objective that separates artifact and authenticity evidence in the detector feature space without requiring paired images or pixel-level manipulation masks. For language output, we use class-conditional Qwen3-VL models to generate complex explanations for fake and real predictions, followed by a GRPO-optimized text simplification model. The proposed methods were trained and evaluated on the challenge subset of XPlainVerse. On the full test split, our submission achieves 0.9349 detection accuracy, 0.5571 explanation score, and a 0.7456 final challenge score.

</details>


### [110] [Perceptually Aligned Evaluation of Style Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10003)
*Yang Deng,Eleftherios Ioannou,David Mould,Steve Maddock,Paul L. Rosin,Yu-Kun Lai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ASTRA提出用于风格迁移的标准化自动评测框架，由数据基准与学习式评分器组成，显著提升与人类偏好的相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风格迁移缺乏可靠评测：没有明确定义的真值，现有自动指标与人类主观偏好相关性差，难以客观比较模型。

Method: 构建ASTRA-Data：包含内容与风格参考图像基准、在基准上生成的多模型风格化结果，以及通过两阶段成对比较的人类标注；据此得到内容保持、风格忠实度与总体偏好的排序“真值”。在此基础上训练ASTRA-Score：输入（内容、风格、风格化）三元组，学习输出与人类排序一致的评分，用于对新模型在基准上的自动评估。

Result: ASTRA-Score与人类排名的相关性显著高于以往指标；ASTRA提供了可扩展的、与人类偏好对齐的风格迁移评测结果。

Conclusion: ASTRA建立了稳健、可标准化的风格迁移评测机制：数据基准+学习式打分器，能自动、可扩展地对模型进行与人类偏好一致的评价。

Abstract: Style transfer lacks a reliable evaluation standard: ground truth is inherently ill-defined, and existing automatic metrics often fail to reflect human preference. This paper introduces ASTRA (Assessment of Style TRansfer Algorithms), an approach for automatic evaluation of style transfer algorithms; it contains two components, ASTRA-Data and ASTRA-Score. ASTRA-Data consists of a benchmark image set of content and style references, a collection of style transfer results generated on the benchmark set, and user study data capturing human judgements through a two-stage pairwise comparison protocol. From these annotations, we derive ranking-based ground truth for content preservation, style fidelity, and overall preference. Based on ASTRA-Data, we construct ASTRA-Score, a learnt evaluator that predicts preference-aligned scores from content-style-stylization image triplets, enabling automatic and scalable evaluation of new models applied to the benchmark set. Experimental results demonstrate that ASTRA-Score achieves substantially higher correlation with human rankings compared to prior metrics. Overall, ASTRA establishes a robust mechanism for standardised evaluation of style transfer methods.

</details>


### [111] [Playing with Kruskal: algorithms for flat and hierarchical watershed cuts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10012)
*Jean Cousty,Laurent Najman,Benjamin Perret,Deise Santana Maia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文综述并统一了基于加权图的分水岭分割框架，给出从图构建到层次分割连通组件的端到端算法管线，涵盖监督/无监督与多种种子策略，旨在作为实现与应用的参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分水岭与最小生成树等优化问题紧密相关，已有高效算法与多种变体，但概念分散、实现细节分布在不同文献中，缺乏系统化、可复现实用的端到端指南。

Method: 在回顾文献的基础上，提出统一的算法流水线：1) 基于图的图像表示构建与边权设定；2) 各类分水岭变体（标准、层次、监督/无监督）建模；3) 种子获取与策略选择；4) 借助MST等优化工具求解；5) 得到最终层次分割并计算连通组件。强调将不同分水岭思想与算法以一致框架整合。

Result: 形成一个紧凑、可理解的参考实现蓝图，覆盖多种分水岭定义、种子计算方式与层次化输出，并指向高效算法（如基于MST）的使用，便于复现与工程落地。

Conclusion: 统一的端到端分水岭分割框架有助于研究者与工程师选择合适变体与实现路径，加速在不同任务中的应用与重实现。

Abstract: In the framework of edge-weighted graphs, watersheds have proven to be linked to well-known optimization problems, as Minimum Spanning Tree, which allowed the design of efficient algorithms for computing (hierarchical) watershed segmentations. In the present article, after reviewing the literature related to watershed segmentation, we present a detailed end-to-end pipeline of algorithms to compute (hierarchical) watershed segmentations, starting from the computation of graph-based image representations, up to the computation of connected components of the final (hierarchical) segmentation. We consider the several variations of watersheds, including their supervised and unsupervised versions, and the various ways of computing seeds, to name a few. For the first time, we bring together all these watershed notions and algorithms in a compact and understandable way. We aim at providing a reference for those interested in employing and reimplementing the watershed segmentation framework for their task at hand.

</details>


### [112] [Scalable Patch-Level Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10013)
*Maximilian Seitzer,Gabriele Trivigno,Antonín Vobecký,Seungeun Yi,Maxime Oquab,Huy V. Vo,Oriane Siméoni,Piotr Bojanowski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出JEM：一种以信息论为基础的学生-教师式自监督学习（SSL）算法，在300M到70亿参数范围内稳定训练，全球/密集任务表现强，分割优于DINOv2，7B规模在显著更少数据下超越DINOv3的全景分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可扩展SSL往往依赖临时拼凑的多目标与稳定技巧，缺乏统一原理。作者希望基于多视图假设，推导出可解释、可扩展且稳定的原则化SSL方法。

Method: 从多视图假设出发构建信息论目标，将目标分解为可解释项：跨视角对应patch表征对齐为主目标，并加入信息保留与结构保留正则。算法为学生-教师框架，在隐空间进行patch级学习（latent patch-level）。

Result: JEM在300M–7B参数范围内稳定训练；在全球和密集探测任务上表现强劲；在分割基准上持续超过DINOv2；在7B下，未用精炼阶段且仅用约1/12数据，仍在全景分割上超过DINOv3。

Conclusion: 可以基于信息论与多视图原则设计出兼具强表征、可解释与训练稳定性的SSL算法；JEM在大规模上验证了这一点，并在多项密集视觉任务上达到或超越现有强基线。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) at scale produces powerful visual representations. However, most scalable SSL methods rely on ad hoc combinations of multiple objectives and stabilization mechanisms. Taking a step back, we ask if we can design a high-performing, yet principled SSL algorithm. Starting from the multi-view assumption, stipulating that task-relevant content is captured by the information common to different views, we construct an information-theoretic objective decomposing into interpretable terms. This derivation yields JEM, a student-teacher method that learns by aligning corresponding patch representations across views, explicitly regularized by information and structure preservation losses. JEM trains stably from 300M to 7B parameters, and, to our knowledge, is the first latent-space patch-level method demonstrated at 7B scale. Across all scales, JEM reaches strong performance on both global and dense probing tasks, on segmentation benchmarks consistently surpassing the DINOv2 algorithm, an influential foundation for today's strongest visual SSL methods. Notably, at 7B parameters, it exceeds the performance of DINOv3 on panoptic segmentation, despite being trained on $12\times$ less data without refinement stages. These results demonstrate that we can indeed design an SSL algorithm that learns strong representations, is principled and stable.

</details>


### [113] [AdSpark: A Large-Scale Dataset and Benchmark for Product-Centric Advertisement Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10047)
*Zhifei Yang,Zhao Jiang,Keyang Lu,Honghe Zhu,Zheng Zhang,Jingjing Lv,Changping Peng,Ching Law,Zhen Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AdSpark 提出面向“产品中心”的广告视频生成的大规模数据集与评测基准，以支持保留细粒度产品身份并进行多镜头叙事的广告生成；提供约30万图-文-视频三元组及结构化广告标注，并构建六维度评测基准；实验显示现有模型在产品保真、多镜头叙事与卖点可视化上存在明显短板，微调后有所改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 产品中心广告视频生成需要同时满足产品身份保真、卖点呈现与多镜头连贯叙事，但该方向缺乏大规模、广告特化的数据与系统性评测，导致研究发展受限。

Method: 1) 构建AdSpark-300K：包含真实与合成子集的约30万参考图—提示词—视频三元组，附带结构化广告标注（产品身份、卖点描述、创意方案、配音脚本）；2) 设计AdSpark-Bench：从视觉质量、产品保真、指令遵循、时序连贯、音频对齐与广告有效性六方面进行诊断性评测；3) 用该基座对代表性模型做评测与在AdSpark-300K上微调验证。

Result: 基于AdSpark-Bench的评测表明：现有代表性生成模型在产品保真、多镜头故事性与卖点可视化方面表现不足；在AdSpark-300K上微调后，指标取得显著提升，验证数据集的有效性。

Conclusion: AdSpark提供了统一的数据集与全面基准，填补广告视频生成领域的数据与评测缺口，为后续研究与模型改进（尤其是产品保真与多镜头广告叙事）奠定基础；数据集将在论文接收后开放。

Abstract: Product-centric advertisement video generation aims to create promotional videos that preserve fine-grained product identity while presenting selling points through coherent multi-shot narratives. However, this emerging task remains underexplored due to the lack of large-scale advertisement-specific datasets and comprehensive evaluation frameworks. To address this gap, we introduce \textbf{AdSpark}, a large-scale dataset and benchmark for product-centric advertisement video generation, based on data from a major e-commerce platform. \textit{AdSpark-300K} contains approximately 300K reference image--prompt--video triplets, comprising a real-world subset and a synthetic subset. Each sample provides structured advertisement annotations, including product identity annotations, selling-point descriptions, creative plans, and aligned audio scripts, enabling models to learn product preservation and advertisement-oriented visual storytelling. We further propose \textit{AdSpark-Bench}, a diagnostic benchmark that evaluates generated advertisements across six dimensions, including visual quality, product fidelity, instruction adherence, temporal coherence, audio alignment, and advertisement effectiveness. Based on AdSpark-Bench, we evaluate representative models, revealing key challenges in product preservation, multi-shot storytelling, and selling-point visualization. Experiments with AdSpark-300K-finetuned models further validate the effectiveness of our dataset. AdSpark provides a unified dataset and benchmark for future research, and we will release the dataset upon acceptance.

</details>


### [114] [From Pixel to Coding: Evaluating the Figure Reproduction Capabilities of MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10066)
*Zijian Chen,Zhengyu Chen,Bohan Liang,Lirong Deng,Yushuo Zheng,Yanwei Jiang,Qi Jia,Kaiwei Zhang,Wenjun Zhang,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FigCodeBench评测框架，系统评估MLLM将复杂图形理解为可执行代码的能力，含多语言、多难度、多维度指标，并在24个模型上发现跨语言与难度存在普遍的非线性性能断崖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准将视觉理解与代码生成割裂评测，且视觉到代码任务多为简化场景，无法检验统一多模态推理与复杂结构复现能力，需要一个真实、系统的“看图-写码复现图形”的评测体系。

Method: 构建FigCodeBench：1) 数据集流水线，6194个实例，覆盖7类功能、4种编程语言；2) 以视觉与代码复杂度建模，将图形复现分为三层难度（含复杂结构推理、不同纵横比、致密几何约束）；3) 评测协议含视觉保真度与语法同构两维度，并验证与MMOS及人工偏好高度一致；4) 在24个专有与开源MLLM上进行大规模实验。

Result: 所有模型在不同语言与难度场景下均出现“非线性性能断崖”；在刚性声明式语言中指标显著下降；评测指标与MMOS/人类偏好高度相关。

Conclusion: FigCodeBench为MLLM图形复现到代码的统一评测提供了标准化数据与指标，揭示当前模型在复杂结构与不同语言下存在系统弱点，为未来模型与训练策略改进提供方向。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities in both visual understanding and code generation. However, existing benchmarks typically evaluate these two modalities in isolation, lacking a dedicated assessment of their unification, i.e., how a model can perceive complex visual structures and synthesize them into precise, executable code. Moreover, current visual code generation benchmarks often rely on simplified layouts within single programming environments, falling short of evaluating true unified multimodal reasoning. To bridge this gap, we propose FigCodeBench, a comprehensive framework for rigorously evaluating MLLMs on figure reproduction, integrating multimodal comprehension and generation. We first design a systematic dataset construction pipeline, resulting in a total of 6,194 instances that cover 7 functional categories and 4 types of programming languages. We further categorize figure reproduction into three tiers with visual and code complexity modeling, specifically targeting complex structural reasoning, varying aspect ratios, and dense geometric constraints. We introduce a multi-dimensional evaluation protocol, encompassing visual fidelity and syntactic isomorphism, that aligns highly with the Mean Machine Opinion Score (MMOS) and human preferences. Based on our framework, we conducted extensive experiments on 24 widely used proprietary and open-source MLLMs (e.g., Gemini 3.1 Pro, GPT-5.4, and Kimi-K2.5), where we observed a universal, non-linear performance cliff across different programming languages and difficulty scenarios for all models, and gained several insights, such as the significant metric decline in rigid declarative languages.

</details>


### [115] [Temporal Residual Bottleneck for Robust Asynchronous Collaborative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10090)
*Melih Yazgan,Ahmed Abouelazm,J. Marius Zöllner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种用于异步协同感知的“时间残差瓶颈”方法：以位姿扭曲后的协作特征为锚，结合Δt条件的xLSTM从历史中提取时间残差信息，并以门控、正则化的方式仅施加必要修正，从而在延迟与丢包下更稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同感知在通信延迟、不同步与丢包下性能下降；多数稳健方法依赖光流对齐或直接特征传输，但当时序对应关系不准时容易误导，破坏静态结构。需要一种在不确定时序下，既能利用历史信息又能避免过度改写可靠特征的机制。

Method: 将异步协同感知建模为时间残差预测：1) 以确定性的位姿扭曲（pose-warped）协作特征作为保守锚点；2) 用Δt条件的xLSTM从可用历史中抽取时间证据，预测残差；3) 通过面向检测器的残差瓶颈，仅以门控与正则化的小幅修正调整锚点特征，再与自车特征融合，降低在对应不确定时覆盖可靠静态结构的风险。

Result: 在DAIR-V2X与OPV2V上，方法在固定/不规则长延迟与丢包条件下显著更稳健；在DAIR-V2X上，相比同步峰值精度略有牺牲，但在更强通信退化下表现更优。诊断实验显示：直接特征传输在存在“神谕式”精确对应时上限更高，但在真实不精确对应下不可靠。

Conclusion: 时间残差融合为异步且不完整的协同感知提供了务实替代方案：以稳健锚点+Δt条件残差+门控正则修正，权衡峰值精度与退化鲁棒性。代码将公开。

Abstract: Collaborative perception extends the sensing range of autonomous vehicles, but its performance degrades when shared features arrive stale or incomplete. Most latency-robust methods compensate delayed collaborator features through flow-guided alignment or direct feature transport. In this work, we formulate asynchronous collaborative perception as temporal residual prediction. Our Temporal Residual Bottleneck keeps a deterministic pose-warped collaborator feature as a conservative anchor and uses a $Δt$-conditioned xLSTM to extract residual temporal evidence from the available history. A detector-facing residual bottleneck then applies only gated, regularized corrections before ego-side fusion, reducing the risk of overwriting reliable static structure when temporal correspondence is uncertain. Experiments on DAIR-V2X and OPV2V show that our method is especially effective under severe fixed/irregular delays and packet drops. On DAIR-V2X, the reported checkpoint trades a small amount of synchronized peak accuracy for better robustness under stronger communication degradation. Controlled diagnostics further indicate that direct feature transport has oracle headroom but can become unreliable when deployed without accurate correspondence. These results support temporal residual fusion as a practical alternative for asynchronous and incomplete collaborative perception. Code will be publicly released at https://url.fzi.de/8dk38.

</details>


### [116] [A Probabilistic Perspective on Wasserstein-Based Evidential Uncertainty for Out-of-Distribution Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10116)
*Arnold Brosch,Abdelrahman Eldesokey,Michael Felsberg,Kira Maag*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出在语义分割的证据深度学习（EDL）中，用Wasserstein目标替代欧氏目标，以单次前向传播获得更可靠的不确定性；在不同骨干上最佳W阶不同，并在多OOD基准上优于同类基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分割模型在部署遇到OOD物体会失败，且常用softmax置信度过度自信；Bayesian方法（MC dropout、深度集成）代价高。EDL能用单次前向传播建模Dirichlet不确定性，但现有EDL的欧氏损失将分布推向单纯形顶点，导致过度自信，无法对不熟悉输入保留不确定性。

Method: 在统一的EDL框架中，将学习目标从欧氏距离改为符合概率单纯形几何的Wasserstein距离（不同阶次W_p）。系统研究不同Wasserstein阶次对语义分割精度与OOD检测的影响。将方法应用于两类骨干：卷积型DeepLabV3+与Transformer型SegFormer，并在SegmentMeIfYouCan基准（含LostAndFound、RoadObstacle21、RoadAnomaly21、Fishyscapes）上评测。

Result: 最佳Wasserstein阶与骨干相关：卷积骨干以二阶最优，Transformer骨干以三阶最优。整体上，该框架在多数指标上超过可比基线，同时保持单次确定性前向传播的效率。

Conclusion: 以Wasserstein目标训练EDL能更好地匹配概率单纯形几何，缓解过度自信并提升OOD检测与分割性能；不同架构需选择不同的W阶，方法在主流OOD分割基准上表现领先且推理高效。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation networks operate on a fixed set of classes and therefore fail when out-of-distribution (OOD) objects appear during deployment, a critical limitation for safety-critical applications such as autonomous driving. Reliably identifying OOD objects requires well-calibrated epistemic uncertainty, yet common softmax-based confidence scores remain overconfident, while Bayesian alternatives such as Monte Carlo dropout or deep ensembles require costly repeated forward passes. Evidential Deep Learning (EDL) offers an efficient alternative by modeling class probabilities as a Dirichlet distribution learned from a single deterministic forward pass. Existing EDL formulations rely on Euclidean objectives that push predictions towards the simplex vertices, encouraging overconfidence rather than preserving uncertainty for unfamiliar inputs. We instead employ Wasserstein-based objectives, which respect the geometry of the probability simplex, and study the influence of the Wasserstein order on segmentation accuracy and OOD detection within a unified evidential framework. We evaluate this framework on a convolutional (DeepLabV3+) and a transformer-based (SegFormer) architecture on the SegmentMeIfYouCan benchmark, including LostAndFound, RoadObstacle21, RoadAnomaly21, and Fishyscapes. Our results show the optimal Wasserstein order is architecture-dependent: second-order objectives dominate on the convolutional backbone, third-order objectives on the transformer backbone, and our framework surpasses comparable baselines on most metrics, with a single deterministic forward pass.

</details>


### [117] [HarnessIR: Harnessing Multimodal Foundation Models for Universal Real-World Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10133)
*Xiangtao Kong,Shuaizheng Liu,Rongyuan Wu,Lingchen Sun,Zhengqiang Zhang,Jinxin Zhao,Yuhui Wu,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HarnessIR：利用多模态基础模型（MFM）作为执行器的一体化真实场景图像复原代理框架，以单次执行+验证迭代替代传统串行工具链；在MiO100上达SOTA，并在真实场景上具强泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界图像退化往往为噪声、模糊、天气等复杂混合退化，难以被“逐退化”地干净分解与处理。现有代理方法依赖按任务分类的单退化模型序列调用，能力受限、误差易累积、难以泛化。需要一种能整体感知、统一执行并具备强泛化能力的IR框架。

Method: 提出HarnessIR五阶段代理框架：1) 感知与诊断：识别退化类型/强度并收集证据；2) 按需调用工具：可辅助获取信息，但不依赖单退化模型链；3) 提示构造：将需求、诊断与证据组织为对MFM的指令；4) 执行：由多模态基础模型一次性完成复原；5) 验证与驱动式细化：对结果进行核验，必要时触发进一步处理与迭代。核心是以MFM充当统一的执行器，单通道复原而非多工具串联。

Result: 在合成基准MiO100上达到SOTA；在复杂真实场景上相比以往代理式IR系统表现更稳健、质量更高，显示出MFM的强泛化与复原能力。

Conclusion: 将MFM“缰束”为IR代理的统一执行器，绕开任务特定模型链的瓶颈，实现单次复原+验证迭代的流程，既在基准上领先，又在真实世界具更强泛化；为Real-IR提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Real-world low-quality images suffer from complex mixed degradations, including but not limited to noise, blur, atmospheric effects, etc. Recent agentic methods usually model real-world image restoration (Real-IR) as a sequential tool calling problem over task-specific single-degradation restoration models. This paradigm, however, is fundamentally limited because complex real-world degradations cannot be cleanly undone degradation by degradation, and the tool used for task-specific models caps the capability of the agent system. In this work, we present HarnessIR, an agentic framework for Real-IR by harnessing a multimodal foundation model (MFM) as the executor. HarnessIR consists of five stages: perception and diagnosis, on-demand tool invocation, prompt composition, execution, and verification-driven refinement. Unlike prior agentic Real-IR methods that rely on tool chains assembled from task-specific models, HarnessIR feeds the restoration requirements, the perceptual diagnosis, and the evidence into an MFM that performs restoration in a single pass, followed by verification stages to determine whether the result warrants further processing. Under our harness, off-the-shelf MFMs handle restoration tasks remarkably well, achieving state-of-the-art results on the widely used MiO100 synthetic benchmark. More importantly, by exploiting the strong generalization ability of MFMs, HarnessIR delivers compelling restoration quality on challenging real-world scenes where previous agentic IR systems often struggle. Codes is available at https://github.com/PolyU-VCLab/HarnessIR.

</details>


### [118] [HeiCo-FOCUS: A Clinically Grounded Dataset for Long-Context Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10156)
*Leon Mayer,Lucas Luttner,Patrick Godau,Kai Fritzsche,Annika Reinke,Leonie Boland,Jule Brandt,Janne Heinecke,Chloe K. Nobuhara,Niklas Holzwarth,Evangelia Christodoulou,Marcel Knopp,Dominik Michael,Pascale Piermarco,Saliq Neyaz,Korhan Derin Özarslan,Jakob Hennighausen,Carlos Aumente-Maestro,Tim Rädsch,Dheeraj Baji,Peter Maximilian Full,Finn Aichholz,Justus Veit Erpenbeck,Linus Finn Schott,Bastian Winkelhausen,Claas de Boer,Bianca Güttner,Anneli Hummel,Gregor Just,Max Kirchner,Chenyang Li,Rozenn Raffaut,Ariel Rodriguez,Danush Kumar Venkatesh,Kevin Wang,Jinjing Xu,Mona Sheikh Zeinoddin,Salman Khan,Thomas M. Pausch,Stefanie Speidel,Danail Stoyanov,Daniel A. Hashimoto,Fiona R. Kolbinger,Thomas G. Weiser,Lena Maier-Hein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HeiCo-FOCUS数据集与评测框架，用于评估VLM在长时程外科视频中保持时序一致性的能力；现有前沿模型整体表现不足，尤其在时间定位上薄弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频-语言模型评估多聚焦短时推理，缺乏对“长时间维度上累积一致性”的系统性测量；外科场景对持续追踪与上下文一致性尤为关键，因而需要一个临床落地且可逐步加大时序与情境难度的评测基准。

Method: 基于海德堡结直肠外科视频构建长时程VQA基准HeiCo-FOCUS：覆盖插入、操作、遮挡与移除等过程中的多物体持续追踪；设计5类能力维度（物体识别、时间定位、信息聚合、事件与流程理解、复杂推理）；通过众包与39位外科专家多阶段标注，形成3万条VQA；提出多轨评测，从单帧到全手术逐级提升时序与上下文要求；对10个前沿VLM开展系统实验与分析。

Result: 仅约半数模型显著优于文本基线；在视频子任务上，事件与流程理解最好（平均准确率56.5%），时间定位最差（平均准确率19.7%）；总体显示长时程一致性与时间推断仍是当前VLM短板。

Conclusion: HeiCo-FOCUS弥补长时程视频理解评测缺口，为外科情境下的时序一致性推理提供高质量基准与逐级评测框架；当前模型仍远未解决该任务，预计该基准将推动能在小时级视频上进行可靠、持续推理的模型发展。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have led to rapid progress in video understanding across a wide range of benchmark tasks. However, existing evaluations largely focus on short-term reasoning, failing to assess a critical capability: maintaining cumulative temporal consistency over extended time horizons. To close this evaluation gap, we introduce HeiCo-FOCUS, a clinically grounded dataset for evaluating long-context video understanding through the task of Foreign Object Contextual Understanding in Surgery. Built on a dataset of Heidelberg Colorectal surgeries, this task requires models to continuously track multiple objects as they are inserted, manipulated, occluded, and removed over procedures lasting up to hours. HeiCo-FOCUS comprises 30,000 visual question answering (VQA) pairs covering five core capabilities: object recognition, temporal grounding, aggregation, event and procedural understanding, and complex reasoning. The dataset was constructed through a rigorous multi-stage annotation pipeline involving large-scale crowd annotation and 39 surgical domain experts to ensure high quality and clinical relevance. To systematically probe model behavior, we introduce a multi-track evaluation framework that progressively increases temporal and contextual demands from single frames to full procedures. Experiments with ten frontier VLMs show that HeiCo-FOCUS tasks are far from solved: only around half of the models clearly outperform a text-only baseline. Across the video tracks, models perform best on event and procedural understanding (mean Accuracy: 56.5% across all models), while temporal grounding remains particularly challenging for all evaluated models (mean Accuracy: 19.7%). We therefore expect HeiCo-FOCUS to serve as a catalyst for the development of models capable of reliable, temporally consistent reasoning over hours-long videos.

</details>


### [119] [BagDINO: Multi-View Baggage Re-Identification with DINOv3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10160)
*Vita Santa Barletta,Danilo Caivano,Rebecca Margiotta,Massimiliano Morga,Davide Pio Posa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 用DINOv3特征并配合BNNeck与LoRA进行参数高效适配，在多摄像头行李重识别任务（MVB基准）上，相比冻结或全量微调，更稳定且有效，适合小数据场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机场托运行李经常被误处理，传统依赖标签的追踪在标签缺失时无法视觉识别，亟需无需标签的跨摄像头视觉重识别方案以提升找回效率。

Method: 将行李重识别建模为多摄像头实例级检索：以DINOv3作为骨干提取视觉表示，在其上接入Torchreid风格的BNNeck re-ID头，并通过LoRA进行参数高效适配；在MVB数据集上以递进式实验对比全冻结、LoRA与全量微调策略。

Result: 在MVB基准上，使用LoRA对DINOv3进行参数高效适配优于完全冻结且更稳定，且在受限训练数据条件下与全量微调相比具有更好的性能-稳定性权衡。

Conclusion: 在小样本与多视角场景中，基于DINOv3的基础模型特征结合LoRA与BNNeck能实现有效、稳定的行李重识别，是比全量微调更实用的参数高效方案。

Abstract: Mishandled checked baggage remains a recurrent issue in airport operations, and current recovery workflows still largely rely on tag-based tracking, which does not directly support visual identification when tag evidence is missing or unavailable. This paper investigates baggage re-identification as an instance-level retrieval problem in a multi-camera setting, leveraging DINOv3 foundation-model representations to match a query image against a gallery of registered baggage images. A Torchreid-style BNNeck re-identification head is placed on top of a DINOv3 backbone, and parameter-efficient adaptation is performed via LoRA. Experiments are conducted on the MVB benchmark using a progressive study that compares a fully frozen backbone against LoRA and fine-tuning strategies. Results indicate that parameter-efficient adaptation of foundation-model features provides an effective and stable approach for multi-view baggage re-identification under limited training data.

</details>


### [120] [Beyond Anonymous Captions: Grounding Character Identity in Video Captioning and Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10163)
*Anas Filali Razzouki,Killian Steunou,Khalil Guetari,Thomas Kling,Mounîm El-Yacoubi,Yannis Tevissen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个结合角色识别、空间指认与任务自适应的“身份感知”视频理解框架，构建基准并证明显式传达“谁在何处”能显著提升视频字幕与以人问答性能；同时用LoRA微调Qwen得到BAC系列，在同规模中表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视频字幕/问答往往将人物当作匿名体，缺乏对角色身份与空间位置的显式建模，导致叙事理解薄弱；现有方法也缺少大规模、具身份标注与空间落点的基准来评测与推动这一方向。

Method: 1) 从LSMDC v2电影片段出发，做人脸检测与演员参考图匹配，实现角色识别与跨帧跟踪；2) 构建带身份绑定框（人脸/人体估计）与文本化坐标的输入；3) 用强基座视觉-语言模型生成身份感知字幕与人物中心问答，人工校验过滤，得到750段带字幕和3000条问答基准；4) 系统研究五种“文本坐标+视觉框”组合在2B/4B/8B及更大模型上的作用；5) 对Qwen 2B/4B/8B进行LoRA微调，形成BAC，用约3.2万段身份感知字幕数据训练。

Result: 结合视觉人脸框与文本坐标的指认策略在不同规模模型上最稳定、显著优于仅用坐标；小模型在未落点时倾向误指已知身份，大模型更能识别UNIDENTIFIED情形。BAC在各规模上均优于同级别家族，BAC-8B在QA上达93.20%总体准确率，仅次于前沿模型GPT-5.6 Sol。

Conclusion: 明确表达“谁在何处”并辅以轻量任务微调，可在不改架构的前提下显著提升身份感知视频理解。论文发布了基准、训练数据、代码与BAC权重，促进该方向研究。

Abstract: Linking people's appearance and actions to character identities is essential for understanding video narratives. We present a framework for identity-aware video captioning and person-centric question answering that combines automatic character identification, explicit spatial grounding, and task-specific adaptation. Starting from LSMDC v2 movie clips, our pipeline matches detected faces to actor reference images, tracks characters across frames, and builds inputs with identity-linked bounding boxes. A strong vision-language model generates identity-aware captions and questions, which are manually verified and filtered to create a benchmark of 750 captioned clips and 3,000 person-centric questions. We study five grounding strategies combining textual coordinates with visual face or estimated person boxes across Video-MLLM families at roughly 2B, 4B, and 8B parameters and larger frontier models. Combining visual face boxes with textual coordinates yields the most consistent performance across scales and significantly improves overall performance over coordinates alone. Smaller models tend to over-assign known identities when the queried person is not grounded, while larger models better recognize such UNIDENTIFIED cases. We introduce BAC by LoRA fine-tuning Qwen models at 2B, 4B, and 8B scales on about 32K identity-aware captioned clips. Across all scales, BAC outperforms every other evaluated model family of comparable size. BAC-8B reaches 93.20\% overall QA accuracy, ranking behind only GPT-5.6 Sol among the frontier models evaluated in our study. Overall, explicitly communicating who is where, together with lightweight task-specific adaptation, substantially improves identity-aware video understanding without changing the underlying architecture. We release the benchmark, training data, code, and BAC checkpoints at https://github.com/momentslab/beyond-anonymous-captions.

</details>


### [121] [Argos: Adapt Rich Geometric Priors for Generalizable Online Scene-Change-Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10181)
*Ruihan Xu,Jiae Yoon,Kaichen Zhou,Ue-Hwan Kim,Luca Carlone*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Argos与Argos-SLAM：利用几何基础模型(GFM)的隐式3D特征，实现联合场景变化检测与3D重建，并在多数据集上训练与评测，显著优于现有方法且可实时用于机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习式方法依赖2D成对特征，易受视角变化、遮挡与噪声影响、泛化差；显式3D方法常需昂贵离线优化，难以实时与跨域。需要一种既稳健又可扩展、能在线工作的场景变化检测与重建方案。

Method: 利用GFMs的隐式3D知识，设计Argos以适配其特征进行联合变化检测与3D重建；构建包含两个人工合成与一个真实世界的大规模基准数据集，进行跨数据集联合训练以提升跨域泛化；提出面向机器人的实时系统Argos-SLAM，实现在线变化检测与具变化感知的4D建图。

Result: 在多个基准上显著超越现有基线：变化IoU最高提升42.01%，F1最高提升27.91%；系统支持可扩展的真实世界动态环境部署与实时运行。

Conclusion: GFMs的隐式3D先验可有效增强变化检测与重建；Argos/Argos-SLAM在准确性与实时性上兼顾，并通过多域训练实现更强泛化，适用于动态环境中的机器人感知与地图构建。

Abstract: Robots operating in dynamic environments require reliable detection of how their surroundings change over time. Existing learning-based methods largely rely on pairwise 2D image features, which struggle under large viewpoint changes and occlusions, are sensitive to noise, and show limited generalization across domains, while explicit 3D approaches typically require costly offline optimization. We show that the implicit 3D knowledge of Geometric Foundation Models (GFMs) provides a strong basis for addressing these limitations. We introduce Argos, which adapts GFM features for joint scene change detection and 3D reconstruction. To address data scarcity and take a step toward a foundation model for scene change detection, we introduce a large-scale benchmark comprising two synthetic datasets and one real-world dataset, and train jointly across diverse datasets to improve cross-domain generalization. We further introduce Argos-SLAM, a real-time system designed for robotics, which performs online change detection and change-aware 4D mapping. Across benchmarks, our framework substantially outperforms existing baselines, with gains of up to 42.01% in change IoU and 27.91% in F1, while supporting scalable deployment in changing real-world environments.

</details>


### [122] [VideoEvolve: Co-Evolving Memory and Retrieval for Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10183)
*Yongchao Xu,Bowen Ye,Jiefeng Gan,Junkai Ma,Wenzhao Li,Sen Tao,Yi Wei,Jiawei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VideoEvolve：一个自进化的长视频理解框架，通过交替强化学习共同进化“记忆构建与检索”，并以瓶颈感知与能力感知反馈引导优化，在多项基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频方法依赖外部记忆，但通常只在“如何检索”上做动态适配，而“记住什么”基本固定；一旦关键细节未被存入，后续很难补救；且训练易过拟合于特定问答集，难以形成可迁移能力。

Method: 1) 从低帧率视频概览出发；2) 记忆进化器：有选择地补充/重写外部记忆；3) 检索进化器：在随时间演化的记忆上进行自适应检索；4) 交替式Agentic RL：冻结其一、优化其一；5) 瓶颈感知反馈(BEF)：判定当前性能瓶颈在“记忆”或“检索”，将优化聚焦于更受限一侧；6) 能力感知反馈(CEF)：避免仅对训练问题过拟合，偏向尚未充分学习但可学的能力维度；7) 将下游推理经验转化为可迁移的能力更新。

Result: 在多个长视频理解基准上进行广泛实验，证明该框架在准确率/鲁棒性等指标上优于现有基线（摘要未给出具体数值）。

Conclusion: 通过联合进化记忆与检索，并以BEF与CEF引导的交替式强化学习，将下游经验沉淀为通用能力，推动从静态长视频系统走向可经验驱动的自我改进多模态智能。

Abstract: Long video understanding increasingly relies on external memory to organize massive visual streams into compact representations. However, most memory-based methods dynamically adapt how information is retrieved for different questions, while largely fixing what is remembered. This mismatch makes missing details costly to recover, whereas stored information is valuable only when it can be reliably retrieved. To address this issue, we propose VideoEvolve, a novel self-evolving framework that jointly evolves memory and retrieval for long video understanding. Specifically, starting from a coarse low-frame-rate overview, VideoEvolve couples a Memory Evolver for selective memory augmentation with a Retrieval Evolver for adaptive retrieval over the evolving memory. We then co-evolve the two Evolvers through alternating agentic reinforcement learning (Agentic RL), updating one while freezing the other. To steer this alternating evolution, Bottleneck-Aware Evolution Feedback (BEF) identifies whether the current bottleneck lies in memory or retrieval and directs optimization toward the more limiting side. Furthermore, VideoEvolve introduces Capability-Aware Evolution Feedback (CEF) to alleviate downstream feedback from over-specializing memory to a fixed set of training questions, shifting training toward underdeveloped yet learnable video capabilities. By integrating Agentic RL with BEF and CEF, VideoEvolve transforms downstream reasoning experience into transferable capability updates, providing a concrete path from static long-video systems toward experience-driven, self-improving multimodal intelligence. Extensive experiments on multiple long video understanding benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of VideoEvolve.

</details>


### [123] [HuLiGen: Human LiDAR Generation from Parametric Body Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10196)
*Salma Galaaoui,Nermin Samet,David Picard*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HuLiGen：从参数化人体模型生成更拟真的人体LiDAR点云，用flow-matching训练的point transformer，实现合成预训练并在LiDAR人体姿态估计上达SOTA，低标注/低数据场景MPJPE降至多50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实人体LiDAR点云采集与标注昂贵稀缺，限制基于LiDAR的人体分析发展；现有仿真数据与真实观测在几何与传感特性上存在域差距。

Method: 以参数化人体模型为条件，训练一个基于Point Transformer的生成模型；采用flow-matching目标学习点云生成分布，使生成点云更贴近真实分布；用生成数据进行“纯合成”预训练LiDAR人体姿态估计（HPE）模型。

Result: 生成的点云较以往仿真更接近真实分布；在LiDAR HPE任务上，使用HuLiGen合成数据进行预训练达到SOTA，在低标注/低数据情形下MPJPE最高下降约50%。

Conclusion: 高保真合成LiDAR点云可显著缓解数据稀缺，提升LiDAR人体姿态估计性能，特别适用于低资源场景；HuLiGen为生成与预训练提供可复用工具与数据。

Abstract: LiDAR point clouds of humans are extremely expensive to collect and annotate, thus represent a scarce resource that hinders the development of human analysis using this modality. To alleviate this scarcity, prior work relies on simulated human LiDAR, but such samples do not fully reflect the geometry and sensing characteristics of real observations. In contrast, we introduce HuLiGen, a generative model that generates human LiDAR point clouds from a parametric body model, using a point transformer trained with a flow-matching objective. We show that our generated point clouds are closer to the real capture distribution. Using HuLiGen to generate synthetic data, we propose a synthetic-only pretraining scheme for LiDAR-based HPE that achieves state-of-the-art performance, with even larger gains in low-annotation and low-data regimes, where MPJPE is reduced by up to 50%. Code, models and generated samples are available at https://github.com/valeoai/HuLiGen.

</details>


### [124] [VolCo: Volumetric Contact for High-Fidelity Human Grasp Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10197)
*Zhuo Chen,Yihua Cheng,Aleš Leonardis,Hyung Jin Chang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Volumetric Contact (VolCo) 与VolCoDiff，通过体素化接触表示与层级式生成模型，实现更精确、稳定、少穿模的手-物体接触重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手-物体接触多局限于对象表面、依赖手工规则，难以从动捕细节恢复真实接触，导致穿透和不合理姿态；需要一种能表达体内接触细节、同时兼顾局部与全局几何的表示与生成方法。

Method: 1) 提出VolCo：将表面接触扩展为3D体素网格集合，层级组织：体素内的局部接触细节与跨体素的全局手部几何。2) VolCoDiff框架：- 局部模块：以物体SDF为条件的3D VAE，建模局部可能的手部配置；- 全局模块：先将体素特征压缩聚合，再用先验引导的扩散模型学习其分布，以协调全局手形。

Result: 在两个基准上取得最先进的穿透与稳定性指标，能生成贴合的抓握，显著减少穿模。

Conclusion: 体素化接触表示配合分层生成策略可更充分利用动捕数据细节，精准恢复手部接触与整体几何，提升抓握真实性与物理可行性；方法通用且可作为后续手-物体交互建模的基础。

Abstract: Accurate contact modeling is fundamental to understanding hand-object interaction, yet existing contact representations are typically restricted to object surfaces and rely on hand-crafted rules to recover contact details, leading to severe penetrations and implausible results. To better exploit the rich detail in motion-capture data, we introduce Volumetric Contact (VolCo), a representation that expands surface points to a set of 3D volumetric grids. VolCo encodes 3D contact that allows precise hand part recovery, and is organized in an inherent hierarchy: local contact details within each volume and global hand geometry across all volumes. Our framework, VolCoDiff, employs two modules to capture local and global features following this hierarchy. For local contact details, we use a 3D variational autoencoder to model the possible hand configurations conditioned on the local object signed distance field (SDF). For global hand geometry, we design a prior-guided diffusion model that learns the distribution of compressed latent features aggregated from the volumetric grids. We evaluate our method on two benchmark datasets and demonstrate state-of-the-art performance in penetration and stability, indicating the capability to generate tight grasps with much less severe penetrations. Our code is available at https://github.com/chzh9311/volco.

</details>


### [125] [Masked Feature Encoding for Large-Scale Whole Slide Image Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10225)
*Haoyu He,Basile Tessier-Cloutier,Yang Wang,Mahdi S. Hosseini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MFE-MIL：在WSI多实例学习中，用特征空间遮罩重建+分类共同训练的轻量MLP适配器，抑制切片内方差并提升下游性能；推理时仅保留适配器与MIL头；在多数据集与多编码器/聚合器上全面提升ACC/F1与多数AUC，并优于多种坐标/空间方法与2DMamba（多数数据集）；在TCGA生存预测上对所有聚合器均提高C-index。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WSI的MIL通常独立提取patch特征再聚合，但染色、扫描仪和局部纹理导致的切片内方差会淹没判别信号，且许多方法依赖坐标、图结构或分割预处理，带来复杂性与域移风险。需要一种无需空间标注/预处理、能在特征层面抑制方差且与现有编码器/聚合器兼容的通用方法。

Method: 提出Masked Feature Encoding for MIL (MFE-MIL)：在预提取的patch特征上引入一个轻量MLP适配器，联合两条分支训练：1) MIL分类头用于滑片级监督，引导适配器学习抑制切片内无关变异；2) 窗口级（按栅格顺序的弱先验）遮罩重建分支，对被遮罩的特征进行重建，作为正则化，无需坐标、图或分割。训练时两目标联合优化；推理时移除重建解码器，仅保留适配器+MIL头。适配器前端支持多种主干编码器与聚合器。

Result: 在CAMELYON16/17、PANDA、TCGA-BRCA四个数据集、四类编码器与多种聚合器的组合中，MFE-MIL几乎在所有设置提升ACC/F1，并在多数设置提升AUC；优于基于坐标的空间方法CAMIL；在与UNI的2DMamba对比中，于四个数据集中的三个获得更高AUC。于五个TCGA生存队列上，对所有聚合器平均C-index均有提升，且提升最为稳定。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 在不依赖坐标/分割的前提下，通过特征空间的遮罩重建与分类协同训练，MFE-MIL有效抑制WSI切片内方差，广泛兼容现有编码器与聚合器，并在分类与生存预测任务上稳定提升性能，推理开销低，具有实践落地价值。

Abstract: Whole slide image (WSI) analysis in computational pathology follows a multiple instance learning (MIL) pipeline where patch embeddings are extracted independently and aggregated for slide-level prediction, but within-slide variance from staining, scanner, and local texture can overwhelm the discriminative signal. We propose Masked Feature Encoding for Multiple Instance Learning (MFE-MIL), a feature-space masking framework that trains a lightweight MLP adapter jointly with a window-based masked reconstruction branch and a MIL classification head. The two objectives are complementary. Classification guides the adapter to suppress within-slide patch variance, while window-based masked reconstruction provides an auxiliary regularizer for the adapted features without using patch coordinates, coordinate graphs, or segmentation preprocessing. The raster patch-extraction order is used only as a weak implicit prior. At inference, the decoder is removed, leaving only the adapter and MIL head. Across CAMELYON16/17, PANDA, and TCGA-BRCA with four diverse encoders, MFE-MIL improves ACC/F1 for nearly all tested aggregator-encoder settings and AUC in most, outperforms coordinate-based spatial methods (CAMIL), and achieves higher AUC than 2DMamba on three of four datasets (UNI). On five TCGA survival cohorts it improves the average concordance index for every aggregator tested, its most consistent gain. Code is available at https://github.com/AtlasAnalyticsLab/MFE-MIL.

</details>


### [126] [Geometry-Supervised Visual Representation Learning for Multi-Phenotype Lesion Interpretation in Medical VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10238)
*Hao Wang,Qiwei Zeng,Shuchang Ye,Jinghao Lin,Yuezhe Yang,Yige Peng,Jinman Kim,Lei Bi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PureVision框架，通过几何监督与解剖引导证据聚合，提升医用VLM在多表型病灶解释（定位与表型表征）上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医用VLM在多表型病灶解读中难以同时把握解剖层级与表型子类关系；仅靠语义监督缺乏几何约束，导致视觉嵌入空间不能保持解剖与表型的结构关系；同时与病灶相关的解剖/表型表征稀疏，模型难以聚合关键诊断证据。

Method: 提出PureVision，包括两模块：1) PureEyes：通过“理想空间分布”进行几何监督，将解剖层级与表型子类关系编码进视觉表示的潜在空间；2) PureNeurons：将视觉表示映射到该潜在空间，依据其位置有选择地聚合病灶特异性的解剖与表型证据，实现解剖引导的证据聚合。整体用于医用VLM多表型病灶解释任务。

Result: 在LIDC-IDRI、CBIS-DDSM、3DReasonKnee三个数据集上，PureVision在VQA与放射报告生成中取得更好的病灶定位（grounding）与表型刻画性能，相比现有方法有一致提升。

Conclusion: 几何监督结合解剖引导的证据聚合可在视觉嵌入空间中保留解剖与表型结构关系，从而显著提升医用VLM对多表型病灶的解释能力。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (VLMs) have shown increasing potential for clinical image interpretation. However, these models still struggle to interpret multi-phenotype lesions whose diagnosis requires the joint assessment of multiple pathological phenotypes. Existing vision-language alignment methods produce visual representations that fail to preserve anatomical hierarchies and relationships among phenotypic subclasses. This stems from their reliance on semantic supervision, which lacks geometric constraints to preserve these relationships in the visual embedding space. Moreover, the sparsity of lesion-related anatomical and phenotypic representations makes it difficult for medical VLMs to capture important diagnostic evidence. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{PureVision}, a geometry-supervised visual representation learning framework for multi-phenotype lesion interpretation in medical VLMs. It combines a geometry-supervised representation learning module, \textbf{PureEyes}, and an anatomy-guided evidence aggregation module, \textbf{PureNeurons}. PureEyes provides geometric supervision through ideal spatial distributions that encode anatomical hierarchies and phenotypic subclass relationships. PureNeurons projects visual representations into the learned latent space, using their positions to selectively aggregate lesion-specific anatomical and phenotypic evidence. Experiments on \textit{LIDC-IDRI}, \textit{CBIS-DDSM}, and \textit{3DReasonKnee} demonstrate that PureVision improves lesion grounding and phenotype characterization in visual question answering and radiology report generation. Code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/purevision-06C2.

</details>


### [127] [LoomSC: Scalable Deep Subspace Clustering with Projector Factorization and Exact Spectral Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10266)
*Nairouz Mrabah,Youssef Melki,Mohamed Bouguessa,Riadh Ksantini,Shakeeb Murtaza,Tehseen Zia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LoomSC，通过投影器分解与精确谱降维，避免显式稠密自表达与全亲和矩阵，达成线性可扩展的子空间聚类；在五个图像基准上显著优于SOTA，并在50万样本上保持≥99.8%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自空间聚类依赖稠密自表达矩阵与全亲和谱聚类，时间与内存在样本数上呈二次或更高增长，难以扩展；需要一种在保证子空间保持与连通性的同时，避免构建大矩阵并降低谱分解规模的方法。

Method: 提出LoomSC：1) 将自表示矩阵表示为两个瘦因子的投影器分解，隐式保持系数；2) 交替使用Procrustes与最小二乘更新，约束样本因子正交；3) 基于投影器支撑构造非负二次亲和；4) 通过精确特征映射把归一化谱问题降为仅与因子宽度相关的特征值问题，无需形成全亲和或样本拉普拉斯；5) 提供关于投影器近似、子空间保持与子空间内连通性的理论条件。

Result: 完整流程在固定维度与迭代预算下，对样本数呈线性时间与内存复杂度；在五个图像聚类基准、15个数据集-指标比较中排名第1或第2，平均准确率比最佳基线高6.66个百分点；合成数据可扩展到50万样本且准确率≥99.8%。

Conclusion: 通过投影器因子化与精确谱降维，LoomSC在不构建大规模亲和矩阵的前提下实现高精度、线性可扩展的子空间聚类，并有理论保证与强实证表现。

Abstract: Dense self-expression matrices and full-affinity spectral clustering limit the scalability of subspace clustering. We introduce the Latent Orthogonal Optimization Model for Subspace Clustering (LoomSC), a framework that addresses both bottlenecks through projector factorization and exact spectral reduction. Motivated by the spectral structure of least-squares regression, LoomSC jointly learns latent features and a projector self-representation through two thin factors. Alternating Procrustes and least-squares updates preserve the sample factor's orthogonality while keeping the coefficient matrix implicit. We construct a nonnegative quadratic affinity that preserves the projector's support. An exact feature map then reduces its normalized spectral problem to an eigenproblem whose dimension depends only on the factor width. Neither the full affinity nor the sample Laplacian needs to be formed. Our analysis quantifies the projector approximation and identifies conditions for subspace preservation and within-subspace connectivity. For fixed dimensions and iteration budgets, the complete pipeline has linear time and memory complexity in the number of samples. Across five image-clustering benchmarks, LoomSC ranks first or second in all 15 dataset-metric comparisons against 9 state-of-the-art baselines. Its mean accuracy exceeds the highest baseline mean by 6.66 percentage points. Synthetic experiments scale to 500,000 samples while maintaining at least 99.8% accuracy.

</details>


### [128] [Video Prediction Policy 2: Predict Better, Act Better](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10270)
*Yanjiang Guo,Haodong Yan,Zhide Zhong,Zhongru Zhang,Qingyuan Yang,Qingzhou Lu,Xiaoyu Chen,Yen-Jen Wang,Shuying Deng,Chenghan Yang,Puzhen Yuan,Chenxin Liu,Tun Ban,Xiang Zhu,Yichen Liu,Kun Feng,Haoang Li,Jianyu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VPP2，一种改进的世界动作模型，通过事件级视频继续预训练、单步视觉规划蒸馏与MoT动作模块，实现开放环境下更准确的视频预测与零样本操控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WAM常在开放环境中产生错误运动预测，导致动作失误，原因包括：基础视频模型未针对操控优化，且直接引入动作通道会削弱泛化。需要一种同时提升视频预测与动作生成零样本能力的方法。

Method: 三步法：1) 构建大规模多样操控视频数据，并配以细粒度字幕，进行事件级视频继续预训练以提升开放式任务泛化；2) 将视频模型后训练并蒸馏为固定预测视野的单步视觉规划器；3) 通过Mixture-of-Transformers动作模块学习隐式逆动力学，将动作学习与视觉规划解耦。

Result: 在开放式视频预测指令跟随上，VPP2-14B比Cosmos3-64B高11.0个百分点；在真实世界零样本ALOHA操控上超过最强基线18.5个百分点；经基准特定后训练后，在LIBERO-Pro、LIBERO-OOD与RoboDojo上达到最高成功率。

Conclusion: 针对WAM在开放环境中的预测与动作失误问题，VPP2通过数据与训练范式创新实现强零样本泛化，在多项视频预测与机器人操控基准上取得SOTA表现，验证了事件级继续预训练、单步规划蒸馏与MoT动作模块的有效性。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) have emerged as an important class of generalist robot policies, aiming to transfer video prediction priors to action learning. However, we find that existing WAMs frequently produce incorrect motion predictions in open-ended environment, leading to erroneous actions. We attribute this limitation to two factors: (1) base video models are not optimized for manipulation, and (2) naively incorporating action components into video models can substantially degrade their generalization capabilities. We introduce Video Prediction Policy 2 (VPP2), a WAM that enables strong zero-shot generalization in both video prediction and action generation. First, we curate a large-scale, diverse dataset of manipulation videos to continue pretraining the base video foundation model. We annotate video clips with detailed captions and perform \textit{event-level} video pretraining to promote generalization across open-ended manipulation tasks. Second, we post-train and distill the video model into a single-step visual planner with fixed prediction horizon. Finally, we introduce action module via a mixture-of-transformers (MoT) architecture to learn implicit inverse dynamics model. Experiments demonstrate three key results: (1) VPP2-14B outperforms Cosmos3-64B by 11.0\% points in video prediction instruction-following success rate on open-ended tasks; (2) VPP2 surpasses the strongest baseline by 18.5\% points in success rate on real-world zero-shot ALOHA manipulation tasks; and (3) following benchmark-specific post-training, VPP2 achieves the highest success rates among evaluated methods on the challenging LIBERO-Pro, LIBERO-OOD, and RoboDojo benchmarks.

</details>


### [129] [$Δ$Representation: Geometry Supervised Representation Learning of Phenotypes via Counterfactual Reasoning for Medical VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10286)
*Hao Wang,Qiwei Zeng,Jinghao Lin,Shuchang Ye,Yuezhe Yang,Yige Peng,Haoyuan Che,Jinman Kim,Lei Bi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ΔRepresentation框架，通过反事实增量表征学习提升医学VLM对病灶表型的表示与定位，在ReXGroundingCT与LIDC-IDRI上显著提高病灶定位与表型表征精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学VLM多用语义对齐强化“病理表型”表示，但病理表型本质上是叠加于正常解剖之上的“增量”变化；直接语义对齐未显式建模相对于对应正常解剖的表型增量，导致表型可分性与可定位性不足。

Method: 构建ΔRepresentation，由两部分组成：1) BaseAnatomy：几何监督的解剖表示学习，利用解剖结构内外的空间关系提供细粒度几何监督；2) ΔPhenotype：反事实增量学习，计算病灶表示与其对应正常解剖表示的差分作为增量，并使相同表型的增量在表征空间聚类。总体在医学VLM中学习“基础解剖+表型增量”的分解表示。

Result: 在ReXGroundingCT与LIDC-IDRI数据集上，ΔRepresentation显著优化病理表型结构化表示，提升病灶grounding与表型刻画（表型分类/区分）准确度，优于语义对齐的基线方法。

Conclusion: 显式建模“相对于正常解剖的表型增量”能更好地结构化病理表型表示，提升医学VLM的病灶定位与表型识别能力；反事实增量学习与几何监督的解剖建模是关键。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (VLMs) have shown increasing potential for radiological image interpretation. Medical VLMs encode radiological images into visual representations that capture both anatomical and phenotypic information for diagnosis. Existing approaches improve pathological phenotype representations through semantic-guided representation alignment. However, pathological phenotypes arise as lesion-specific visual changes superimposed on underlying normal anatomy. Such semantic alignment approaches fail to model the phenotype-specific increment relative to the corresponding normal anatomical representation. To address this gap, we propose \textbf{$Δ$Representation}, a visual phenotype representation learning framework based on counterfactual reasoning for medical VLMs. It comprises \textbf{BaseAnatomy}, a geometry-supervised representation learning module, and \textbf{$Δ$Phenotype}, a counterfactual incremental representation learning module. BaseAnatomy provides fine-grained geometric supervision through spatial relationships across and within anatomical structures. $Δ$Phenotype computes the representation increment between lesion representations and their corresponding normal anatomical representations, and supervises increments associated with the same phenotype to cluster in the representation space. Experiments on \textit{ReXGroundingCT} and \textit{LIDC-IDRI} demonstrate that $Δ$Representation effectively structures pathological phenotype representations and improves lesion grounding and phenotype characterization accuracy in medical VLMs. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/deltarep-CF6D.

</details>


### [130] [TouchScale: 500 Hours of Human Vision and Touch for Visual-Tactile Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10288)
*Dayou Li,Hao Wang,Qianqian Yang,Zihao Zhu,Haoquan Fang,Ziyao Zeng,Yan Han,Zihan Wang,Yan Wang,Baoru Huang,Dilin Wang,Kenji Shimada,Yiyue Luo,Manling Li,Teresa Lv,Mustafa Mukadam,Rakesh Ranjan,Ruohan Zhang,Qi He,Changliu Liu,Xu Chen,Marco Pavone,Bangya Liu,Jiachen Li,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Zhiwen Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TouchScale 是一个500小时的统一穿戴式采集的大规模视觉-触觉人类交互数据集，提供同步的第一视角RGB-D、手腕RGB和双手高密度触觉数据，显著提升零样本触觉预测、动作识别与机器人操控性能，并验证数据规模与一致传感的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身学习大量依赖自我视角视频，但视频缺乏触觉与压力信息；现有视觉-触觉数据集体量小、传感器与标注不一致，难以隔离规模效应。作者希望提供一个大且一致的视觉-触觉资源，量化规模与统一感知对感知与操控的增益。

Method: 构建TouchScale数据集：500小时、约2K任务描述，使用单一统一的可穿戴平台采集，同步记录第一视角RGB-D、手腕RGB与双手全手高密度触觉；提供重建物体模型。以该数据进行：1) 训练触觉预测模型并评估在未见触觉传感器上的零样本接触IoU；2) 视觉编码器预训练后在三项动作识别基准上对比；3) 在机器人策略上进行视觉-触觉中期训练并评估实机操控任务；4) 做数据规模消融以观察随数据量增长的趋势。

Result: 在未见触觉传感器上的零样本接触IoU由0.134提升至0.383；基于该数据集预训练的视觉编码器在三项动作识别基准上取得最高准确率；用于机器人策略中期训练时，四项接触密集操作任务的平均成功率由22.5%提升至57.5%；在固定传感与采集协议条件下，随着数据规模增加，零样本触觉预测与机器人成功率总体呈上升趋势。

Conclusion: 规模化且传感一致的人类视觉-触觉数据显著促进感知与机器人操控泛化；TouchScale将公开其同步视觉-触觉数据与重建物体模型，为可扩展的视觉-触觉学习提供基础。

Abstract: Large-scale egocentric human interaction data is becoming an important source of physical supervision for embodied learning, yet video alone leaves the contact and pressure that characterize physical interaction unrecorded. Recent visual-tactile datasets provide this missing supervision, but their synchronized tactile data remain far smaller in volume than human video. Moreover, the largest resources often merge recordings from different sensors or annotation procedures, which makes the effect of data scale difficult to isolate. We therefore introduce TouchScale, a 500-hour dataset of contact-rich human interaction recorded with a single unified wearable setup. Its approximately 2K predefined task descriptions span everyday activities and structured manipulation, and each recording temporally aligns egocentric RGB-D video with wrist RGB video and dense full-hand bimanual tactile measurements. Compared with prior tactile data, training on the full TouchScale raises zero-shot contact IoU on data from an unseen tactile sensor from 0.134 to 0.383. Pretraining a visual encoder on TouchScale also yields the highest action recognition accuracy on three benchmarks among the compared visual-tactile datasets. Used for visual-tactile mid-training of a robot policy, TouchScale improves the average real-world success rate across four contact-rich manipulation tasks from 22.5% to 57.5%. With the sensor and collection protocol held fixed, both zero-shot tactile prediction and robot success show an overall upward trend as more TouchScale data is used. These results suggest that human visual-tactile data collected at scale with consistent sensing benefits both perception and robot manipulation. We will publicly release TouchScale, including all synchronized visual-tactile recordings and reconstructed object models, to support future research on scalable visual-tactile learning.

</details>


### [131] [On the Necessity of Attention-FFN Split in Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10303)
*Junhyeok Kim,Jinyeong Kim,Jae Wan Park,Seong Jae Hwang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出将Attention与FFN合并为统一模块AttenFeed，构建统一ViT（uViT），用以检验传统“注意力-前馈交替”结构是否必要。结果显示在小模型规模下，僵化的二分结构会因参数分配刚性而抑制性能；uViT在多数据集与尺度上提供更优或更稳表现，并作为分析工具揭示该结构的归纳偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准ViT严格交替堆叠注意力与FFN，形成固定的功能分工与参数配比，但这种二分带来的归纳偏置是否合理并未被系统检验。作者动机是：1) 评估该二分是否在不同规模下限制模型能力；2) 提供一种消融与对照手段，分离“功能分离”与“参数配置”的影响；3) 为ViT架构设计提供更理论化的分析工具。

Method: 提出AttenFeed统一模块，融合注意力的全局信息交互与FFN的非线性变换于单一单元；以此堆叠构建uViT，替代传统注意力-FFN交替。将uViT作为“放松二分假设”的对照组，与标准ViT在多数据集与多模型规模上进行系统比较，以量化二分结构的影响。

Result: 实验显示：在小规模模型中，传统二分结构因参数在注意力与FFN间的刚性分配而导致性能受限；uViT在这些设定下取得更好或更稳定的结果。跨数据集与尺度的对比支持上述结论，并表明统一模块能更灵活地利用参数容量。

Conclusion: Attention-FFN二分并非必要，尤其在小规模模型中可能有害。AttenFeed与uViT不仅提高了实际表现，也作为分析工具帮助理解与量化ViT二分结构的归纳偏置，为更灵活的Transformer架构设计提供理论与实践依据。

Abstract: The standard Transformer architecture relies on a rigid pattern that alternates Attention and Feed-Forward Network (FFN) layers. Despite its widespread adoption, the inductive bias imposed by this strict separation has not been systematically examined. In this work, we investigate the necessity of the Attention-FFN dichotomy in Vision Transformers (ViTs). To facilitate this analysis, we introduce the AttenFeed module, a unified component that integrates the functional properties of both Attention and FFN. Based on this module, we devise the unified Vision Transformer (uViT), which replaces the conventional alternating Attention-FFN structure with a sequence of AttenFeed modules. We then use uViT as a control group that relaxes the Attention-FFN dichotomy of the standard ViT and systematically compare the two models across multiple datasets and model scales. Our experiments reveal that the Attention-FFN dichotomy can hinder performance at smaller model scales due to the rigid parameter allocation of ViTs. The AttenFeed module and uViT serve as new analytical tools for understanding the Attention-FFN structure and offer theoretical insights into the heuristically designed architecture of conventional ViTs.

</details>


### [132] [One-Shot Adaptive Segmentation For Scientific Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10306)
*Tejaswi V. Panchagnula,Allison M. Davis,Fengqing Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练的一次性参考图像条件分割框架：用单张标注图像适配通用视觉模型到科学影像分割，显著提升显微和沸腾数据集mIoU，并在胸片上保持相当表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学成像分割常需大量标注与任务特定训练，跨成像模态/实验条件泛化差。希望在不重新训练的情况下，快速把通用视觉基础模型适配到特定科学任务。

Method: 以单张带标注的参考图像进行一次性（one-shot）专化：1) 提取DINOv3特征；2) 进行背景自适应的特征正交化，抑制与伪影/背景相关的特征方向；3) 通过余弦相似度在目标图上定位候选区域；4) 将候选区域输入SAM进行精细分割。

Result: 在红细胞显微镜与结构照明显/池沸腾数据集上，相比最强基线mIoU分别提升5.91%与78.62%；在胸部X光数据集上达到可比的性能。

Conclusion: 一次性参考条件可有效将通用视觉基础模型适配到专门的科学分割任务，在多种成像场景下无需训练即可取得优异或相当的结果。

Abstract: Scientific image segmentation methods rely on extensive annotation and task-specific training, limiting adaptation across imaging modalities and experimental conditions. We present a training-free, one-shot framework that specializes vision foundation models using a single annotated reference image. The framework combines DINOv3 representations with background-adaptive feature orthogonalization to suppress artifact-related feature directions, after which cosine similarity localizes candidate regions for SAM segmentation. We evaluate the framework on red-blood-cell microscopy, structured-illumination pool boiling, and chest radiography. Relative to the strongest baseline, the proposed method improves mean IoU by 5.91% and 78.62% on the microscopy and pool-boiling datasets, respectively, while achieving comparable performance on chest radiographs. These results demonstrate that one-shot reference conditioning can adapt general-purpose vision models to specialized scientific segmentation tasks.

</details>


### [133] [From Digital Human Interactions to Physics-Based Humanoid Skills: Physics-Grounded Post-Training of Interaction Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10322)
*Kerui Chen,Jianrong Zhang,Kai Lv,Hehe Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DIGHT：将文本条件的交互生成器与人形跟踪策略协同自适应，使生成的双人交互既可被物理跟踪执行又更符合交互意图。用物理仿真的偏好信号（可执行性与交互保真度）对生成器做基于扩散的偏好对齐，再用对齐后的参考动作微调跟踪器，显著提升物理可信与交互可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双人交互生成多依赖把参考动作经物理跟踪转为可执行轨迹，但受限于跟踪能力：很多参考动作不可执行，数据利用率低；即使跟上了也常不物理可信或不符合预期互动。需要一种能同时考虑可执行性与交互保真、并能与物理跟踪策略协同改进的生成框架。

Method: 提出DIGHT协同框架：1) 用固定跟踪器在仿真中执行多个文本条件的交互候选；2) 从回放构造基于物理的偏好，分为两类：可执行性（跟踪误差、摩擦/打滑、漂浮）与交互保真（从仿真力反馈度量接触的发生、位置、时长、力幅）；3) 不将多指标压成单一奖励，而用“物理解耦”的扩散DPO对齐预训练生成器，保留准则特异的监督且无需对仿真器求导；4) 用对齐后的生成器输出的参考动作来微调跟踪策略，提升生成-执行兼容性。

Result: 在大量实验中，方法提升了生成动作的物理可信度与交互可靠性；相较基线，候选更易被跟踪执行，接触事件更符合目标的发生频率、位置、时长与力幅，整体交互更稳定、失真更少。

Conclusion: 通过联合对齐生成器与跟踪器，并以物理可执行性与接触保真为导向的偏好学习，DIGHT显著改进了双人交互生成的可执行性与真实性，提供了一种无需可微仿真的高效对齐范式。

Abstract: Recent methods have made promising progress in generating interactions between two humanoids, largely relying on physics-based tracking policies to convert digital reference motions into executable trajectories. However, limited tracking capabilities restrict the range of reference motions that can be successfully executed, reducing data utilization. Moreover, even successful tracking does not guarantee physically plausible responses or faithful realization of the intended interactions. In this paper, we introduce DIGHT, a co-adaptive framework that couples a Digital human Interaction Generator with a Humanoid Tracking policy. Our DIGHT first executes multiple text-conditioned interaction candidates in simulation using a fixed tracker. It then constructs physics-grounded preferences from the resulting rollouts, covering both general executability and interaction fidelity. Rather than collapsing these signals into a single scalar reward for candidate ranking, we align the pretrained generator using physics-decoupled diffusion direct preference optimization (DPO), preserving criterion-specific supervision without differentiating through the simulator. To improve executability, preference pairs are derived from tracking error, friction, and floating. Additionally, to improve interaction fidelity, we propose to incorporate force feedback from simulator as a measure of contact fidelity and construct preferences over contact occurrence, location, duration, and force magnitude. The aligned generator then supplies reference motions for fine-tuning the tracker, improving compatibility between generation and physical execution. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach not only improves the physical plausibility of generated motions but also enables more reliable and faithful humanoid interactions in simulation.

</details>


### [134] [Performance at What Cost? A Sustainability-Aware Performance Index for Cell and Nucleus Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10324)
*Eiram Mahera Sheikh,Alaa Tharwat,Wolfram Schenck*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SAPI指标，将分割性能、能耗与模型规模合成一体，系统评估19个零样本与16个少样本可微调模型，发现“大而强”不必然带来成比例分割提升，资源—性能权衡需显式量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细胞/细胞核实例分割的预训练与基础模型在架构、预训练目标、参数量与推理/后处理等方面差异巨大。尽管大模型具备更强零样本能力，却带来更高能耗、内存与碳排。社区缺乏一个兼顾性能与可持续性的统一比较框架与指标。

Method: 提出可配置的可持续感知性能指数SAPI，将分割指标（如质量得分）、能耗（GPU/CPU/RAM软件监测估算）与模型大小加权融合；在CellBinDB六个数据集上基准：19个模型做零样本推理；16个模型做少样本适配（冻结编码器与全参微调两种）；比较不同架构、数据集与适配策略下的SAPI与仅性能排序的差异。

Result: 更大、更耗算的模型并未稳定带来与资源投入成比例的分割质量提升；少样本适配能提升部分模型但收益与代价强依赖架构与数据，导致SAPI排名与仅看性能的排名显著不一致。

Conclusion: SAPI为生物医学图像分割提供了兼顾准确性与可持续性的实用评估框架，促进在能耗与资源受限情境下做出更可及且更环保的模型选择。

Abstract: Pretrained models for cell and nuclear instance segmentation differ substantially in architecture, pretraining data and objectives, parameter count, inference strategy, adaptation requirements, postprocessing pipeline, and computational demand. Large pretrained and foundation models are increasingly adopted because of their strong zero-shot capabilities, but their use also imposes greater energy consumption, memory requirements, computational demands, adaptation costs, and operational carbon emissions. Whether these additional demands are justified by meaningful gains in segmentation performance remains unclear. We address this question by introducing the Sustainability-Aware Performance Index (SAPI), a configurable metric that combines segmentation performance, energy consumption, and model size. We benchmark 19 pretrained and foundation models across six CellBinDB datasets under zero-shot inference and evaluate 16 fine-tunable models using few-shot adaptation with both frozen encoder and full-model fine-tuning. We estimate energy consumption for GPU, CPU, and RAM using software-based monitoring tools. Our results show that larger and more computationally demanding models do not consistently achieve proportionate improvements in segmentation quality. While few-shot adaptation benefits several models, the gains and resource costs vary considerably across architectures, datasets, and adaptation strategies, causing SAPI-based rankings to differ from rankings based on performance alone. This study provides a practical framework for comparing segmentation models more comprehensively and supports more computationally accessible and environmentally responsible model selection in biomedical image analysis.

</details>


### [135] [How Private is Private? A Comparative Study for Face De-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10334)
*Hui Wei,Hao Yu,Hui Kuurila-Zhang,Guoying Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出统一的FDeID评测基准与指标：数据集UtilFace和分层指标HiFD，解决现有评测碎片化与不可比问题，并据此系统比较多类方法、揭示权衡与失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人脸去识别研究在评估上支离破碎：不同数据集、指标与不完整标注导致难以跨方法比较，尤其在不同“实用性”维度（如关键点、表情、质量）之间缺乏统一框架。需要一个兼顾隐私与多层实用性的标准化评测协议。

Method: 数据侧：构建UtilFace，基于四个大规模人脸集，通过按身份清洗、分辨率增强与分层筛选，保证人口统计平衡与身份多样性。指标侧：提出HiFD，一种一致性范式的分层评测，将原图与去识别人脸输入预训练估计器，比较输出一致性以度量“身份抑制”与多级实用性（L1宏观、L2微观、L3难感知）及图像质量；最终用加权调和平均聚合为可配置、可解释的单分数。

Result: 在统一协议下，对对抗式、GAN式与扩散式FDeID方法进行全面对比，揭示此前协议下不可见的性能权衡与失败模式；提供可复现实验与工具链。

Conclusion: 统一的数据基准与分层指标能系统量化隐私-实用性-质量三者关系，促进可比、可复现实验与方法改进；公开发布UtilFace与HiFD以推动隐私保护人脸分析研究。

Abstract: Face de-identification (FDeID) has emerged as a critical privacy-preserving technology, yet its evaluation remains fundamentally fragmented. Existing protocols rely on inconsistent metrics, heterogeneous datasets, and partial annotation coverage, so methods targeting different utility dimensions, such as landmark versus expression preservation, are reported on different benchmarks under different metrics, rendering cross-method comparison infeasible. We revisit FDeID evaluation from both the data and metric perspectives. On the data side, we introduce UtilFace, a curated, demographically balanced benchmark with high identity diversity, assembled from four large-scale face datasets through identity-aware cleaning, resolution enhancement, and stratified filtering.
